===== PREVIOUS REVIEW =====
# OSStack Session adoption 试点：最终独立复审（纯静态）

我没有运行任何代码或测试。"5881 通过""新增聚焦回归通过""SIGKILL 通过"都是你提供的观察，不是我的结论。行号取自你提供的带行号文件和摘录。

## 1. 结论

**对"本地、默认关闭、有界 opt-in 的 existing awaiting_decision Session 采纳 + observed_turn"这一范围：通过，可合入。** 带限制，不是生产资格。

- **P0：无。**
- **P1：无。**
- **P2：3 项。** 其中 2 项是运维/文档缺口，1 项是小的代码加固。
- **P3：若干**，不阻塞。

我没有找到能击穿以下不变量的路径：重复派发、撤权后派发、越权代答、伪造完成证明、committed unknown 被自动重发或升级清除。

## 2. 逐项处置核验

| 处置 | 结论 | 依据 |
|---|---|---|
| P1-1 在途被降为 unknown | 成立 | `activeSessionSends` 自 admission 起连续持有同一 messageId，覆盖 CAS 到执行。`refresh` 对"committed+accepted+live"不调 `reconcile`（store:500）。`recordCompletion` 与 turn 同事务写 action/item=applied（store:467-468）。 |
| P1-2 来源失败污染 Wait | 成立 | 故障分类为 `unavailable` / `identity`。unavailable 只写去重事件。identity 吸收。非故障错误经 `attention.write` 事务回滚。 |
| P1-3 谱系 | 成立 | `tableIntroductions` 由 `MIGRATIONS[].sql` 动态提取（db.ts:103-105）。`sqlite_sequence` 不属于 experimental 前缀，不会误报。 |
| P2-1 admission 空转 | 成立 | admission 前有只读预检（host:167-168）。异步 admission 后在同步 CAS 前重读授权（:178-180）。退避 1→60 秒，每种原因一条事件。 |
| P2-2 终态与 stop | 成立 | `completed`/`failed` 吸收；首个 stop_intent 保留；stop 上限 8。daemon `stop` 要求 `activeSessionSends.get(session)` 属于该 Mission 的 message 集合（daemon:4348-4352）。 |
| P2-3 对象探测 | 成立 | 非 adopt 路径先 `readableMission`，缺失与无权统一为 `permission_denied`。adopt 先查 scope read。 |
| P2-3 全局 action_id | 契约保留 | 重复 ID 在 `reserve` 处因 payload 不同抛 `action_id_conflict`，整事务回滚，不读出他人回执。 |
| P2-4 取消回执 | 成立 | read model、`session.send`、`attention.answer` replay、`abandon` 都走 `isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch`。坏证明显示 `dispatch_inconsistent`。 |
| P2-5 写放大 | 成立 | `observed_at` 至少间隔 60 秒才更新（store:533）。no-op UPDATE 带 `AND state<>?` 条件。 |
| P2-6 / P2-8 | 文档化 | 与代码一致。一个小遗漏见 P2-C。 |
| P2-7 摘要口径 | 成立 | `decide`/`defer`/`refresh`/`commitDispatch`/`reserve` 都对 parse 后内容取摘要。 |
| 升级 P2-1/2 | 成立 | allow-list 守卫；schema<47 却有 phase 表则拒绝（port:132-133）。 |
| 升级 P2-3 | 部分成立 | 守卫在 stop 前有效；错误分类与 draining 后续有缺口，见 P2-B。 |

## 3. 指定交互场景

- **旧端点 × 修复**
  - `attention.answer` 重放：in-flight 返回 `in_flight`；取消返回取消状态；committed 未知时 `reconcile` 只写 `uncertain`，不执行。
  - `session.send` 重放不会进入 execute。
  - `executeReservedSessionTurn` 的 phase 守卫不受功能开关影响。
  - 守卫失败时 `lease` 初值为 `admittedLease`（daemon:929），所以 finally 能释放。
  - 不同 action_id 的 `attention.answer` 在 `session_busy` 或 `stale_item` 处被拦。
- **回滚**
  - 关功能不抹 reserved，会继续拦手工指令，guide 已写明。
  - 二进制回退：v47 库会被旧构建拒绝（db.ts:123-127），升级端口也不还原数据库。
  - guide 没有说明二进制回退不受支持，见 P2-C。
- **精确 message 取消**
  - 取消、结束观察、后续手工指令三种情况下都只按 Mission 自己的 message 集合 abort。
  - 提交与取消都是同步事务，不会交错。
  - 取消回执的措辞问题见 P3-1。
- **来源错误**
  - 瞬时失败不污染 Wait；identity 吸收，原 native id 回来也不恢复。
  - 但完成事务内的来源错误会回滚真实回合，见 P2-A。
- **派发恢复**
  - CAS 是 `UPDATE … WHERE phase='reserved'` 加 `changes===1` 加一次性 WeakSet 许可。
  - 重启只列 reserved；committed 永不再列；撤权在 CAS 前重读。
- **完成证明**
  - 与 `completeInboundTurn` 同事务。
  - 版本为 `reservation_version+1`，action/message/turn 全绑定。
  - 取消或终态后仍会写账本，但 Mission 不会被改成 completed。
- **授权**
  - 每个 Mission RPC 先要求 scope read。
  - cancel 要求 stop，且是 owner 或 operator。
  - endObservation 允许只读 owner。
  - 轮换凭据不撤销 grant；撤销 principal 行才阻断派发。
  - 追踪只记录 method。
- **迁移与 drain**
  - 46→47 的 DDL 在同事务内；未来 48 被拒。
  - 停机后在独占锁内再检；committed 且 applied、或取消证明严格成立，才清除 hold。

## 4. 未解决问题

### P2-A 完成事务内的 `source()` 可回滚已完成的真实回复

- **位置**：`engineering-store.ts:457`（`recordCompletion` 调 `this.source(mission)`）；`daemon.ts:1068`、`:1076-1081`。
- **触发**：Worker 刚完成回复，仓库路径此刻 `realpathSync` 失败或身份变化。例如 Worker 清理了 worktree，或挂载瞬断。
- **影响**：
  - `EngineeringSourceFault` 使 `completeInboundTurn` 一并回滚。
  - 外层 catch 把 message 标为 failed，返回 `delivery_uncertain`。
  - 回复文本和 turn 丢失，Mission 进入 `needs_reconciliation`。
  - 该 committed 行成为永久升级 hold（见 P2-C）。
  - 安全方向仍是 fail-closed，没有重发。
- **修复**：`recordCompletion` 只用 `SessionRepository.latestSnapshot` 加任务/运行/native 绑定校验，不碰文件系统；仓库可用性留给 `refresh` 处理。
- **回归**：在完成事务中注入 `realpath` 失败。断言 turn 与 applied 仍落库、proof 写入，之后 `refresh` 报 unavailable，Mission 不进 `needs_reconciliation`。

### P2-B drain 的错误分类与"拒绝后 draining 残留"未验证

- **位置**：`upgrade-native-port.ts:94-97`；`daemon.ts:1381-1393`；`upgrade-native.test.ts:25`、`:66-70`。
- **触发**：真实 schema47 daemon 有 holds>0 时返回 `drained:false`，端口先做 `drained: z.literal(true)` 解析，抛 ZodError。`attention_dispatch_holds !== 0` 的专门检查因此对真实 daemon 基本不可达。
- **影响**：
  1. 仍然 fail-closed，且在 stop 前；但错误原因不是 `upgrade_unknown_actions`。fake daemon 返回"drained:true 且 holds=1"，是真实 daemon 不会产生的组合，所以测试没覆盖真实组合。
  2. `daemon.drain` 把 `ctx.draining` 置 true 后拒绝升级，daemon 保持 draining：reserved 不再派发，`decide` 返回 draining。是否有人调用 `daemon.resume` 取决于升级协调器，我没看到该代码。
- **修复**：先检查 holds 再 parse `drained`，或在 schema 中去掉 `drained` 字面量约束；确认协调器在拒绝后会 resume 或明示需要手动 resume。
- **回归**：用真实 `startDaemon` 加 reserved 行跑 drain。断言原因为 `upgrade_unknown_actions`，daemon 未被 stop，resume 后 reserved 恢复派发。

### P2-C 运维契约缺口：未知 committed 永久阻断升级，二进制回退未声明

- **位置**：`engineering-upgrade-guard.ts:14-20`；`daemon.ts:1387-1391`；`upgrade-native-port.ts:133`；`engineering-session-adoption.en.md` 的 "Disable and recover"。
- **触发**：
  - 存在 committed 但未 applied 的行，例如 failed、重启后 unknown、或已被 `abandon-receipt` 处置。守卫忽略 receipt resolution，所以永远计为 hold。
  - 或在迁移后希望回退二进制。
- **影响**：
  - 该 home 的所有后续托管升级都被拒，试点内没有受支持的出口，只能手改数据库。
  - guide 没有写这一点，也没有写 v47 库无法被旧构建打开。
  - 这与"未知 committed 不被升级清除"一致，但缺少运维说明。
- **修复**（二选一）：
  - 在 guide 和 runtime-contract 中明写"在试点内这是预期的 fail-closed 终态，需要人工、带证据的离线处置"，并说明二进制回退的限制。
  - 或设计"带操作员证据的显式放行"，需要独立评审，不建议塞进本 PR。
- **回归**：目前至少要有文档检查。若实现放行，需覆盖"resolution 存在但 message 未 applied 仍保持 hold"和"不得自动放行"。

### P3（不阻塞）

1. `engineering-host.ts:80-85`：纯"取消未派发"且无 live 调用时，也写 `stop_unknown`（文案"prior worker exit is unknown"）。实际没有 Worker 被派发，应区分，避免误导操作员。
2. `daemon.ts:869`：错误码白名单缺 `dispatch_inconsistent`，会落入通用失败路径。功能安全，只是错误码不清晰。
3. CLI：值以 `--` 开头的答案文本（`engineering-command.ts:105`）会被拒，需要改用 `--input`。
4. `adopt` 中 `executor_not_qualified` 先于写权检查（host:45），只对该 scope 的读者可见，影响很小。

## 5. 证据局限

- **一律是静态阅读。** 没有任何执行证据，包括你给出的全量测试结果、聚焦回归、两个 SIGKILL 场景。fixture adapter 在进程内，不证明 Worker 进程组被包含。
- **SIGKILL 窗口。**
  - 只覆盖 admission 阶段和 adapter 已调用后。
  - 未覆盖"CAS 提交 → adapter 调用"之间、`completeInboundTurn` 事务中、cancel 提交 → abort 之间。
  - 未覆盖"已取得仓库 writer lock、CAS 之前"被杀。重启后能否恢复，取决于未提供的 `acquireRepoWriteLock(recover_stale:false)` 对遗留 lock 的语义。
  - SIGKILL 不等于断电，FULL 与文件系统行为未验证。
- **未提供的上下文**，我没有假定其正确：
  - 升级协调器（拒绝后是否 resume）。
  - `AttentionObserver` / `AttentionExperience`（是否在 in-flight 时调用 `AttentionActions.reconcile`，P1-1 的修复依赖它们不这样做）。
  - `SessionRepository.retryFailedMessage` 的调用方（permit 守卫应当兜底，但未验证）。
  - 中文 guide、生成的 JSON schema、`local-ledgers-daemon.test.ts`。
  - `daemon.ts` 其余部分，只依据摘录和补丁推断。
- **环境。** 真实 Provider 回合因 API key 缺失未覆盖。未验证：同 UID 恶意 Worker、多租户、远程宿主、物理断电。费用上限未声明，用量缺失记为 unknown，与代码一致。
- `disposition.md` 的"最终复审"一节仍是占位符，本报告可作为其输入追加。追加时应保留"静态复审、未执行测试"的限定。

107:     this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "read"); return mission;
108:   }
109:   private readable(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, missionId: string): EngineeringMission { return this.readableMission(principal, missionId); }
110:   private steer(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, mission: EngineeringMission): void {
111:     this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "read"); this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "write");
112:     if (mission.owner_principal_id !== principal.principal_id) throw Error("permission_denied");
113:   }
114:   private controlBudget(missionId: string): void {
115:     if ((this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM engineering_requests WHERE mission_id=? AND operation NOT IN ('cancel','endObservation')").get(missionId) as { n: number }).n >= 256) throw Error("budget_exhausted");
116:   }
117:   private cas(mission: EngineeringMission, revision: number): void {
118:     if (mission.revision !== revision) throw Error("version_conflict");
119:     if (terminal.has(mission.state)) throw Error("mission_terminal");
120:   }
121:   private clock(now: number): void { if (!Number.isSafeInteger(now) || now < 0) throw Error("invalid_clock"); }
122:   private event(missionId: string, kind: string, actor: string | null, payload: unknown, now: number): number {
123:     return Number(this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO engineering_events(mission_id,kind,actor,payload_json,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)").run(missionId, kind, actor, JSON.stringify(payload), now).lastInsertRowid);
124:   }
125:   private bump(missionId: string, state: EngineeringMission["state"], now: number): EngineeringMission {
126:     this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_missions SET state=?,revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE mission_id=?").run(state, now, missionId);
127:     return this.getMission(missionId);
128:   }
129:   private request(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, requestId: string, operation: string, payload: unknown): StoredRequest | undefined {
130:     const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_requests WHERE principal_id=? AND client_session_id=? AND request_id=?").get(principal.principal_id, principal.client_session_id, requestId) as StoredRequest | undefined;
131:     if (row && (row.operation !== operation || row.payload_hash !== attentionDigest(payload))) throw Error("action_conflict");
132:     return row;
133:   }
134:   private saveRequest(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, requestId: string, operation: string, payload: unknown, missionId: string, result: unknown, now: number): void {
135:     this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO engineering_requests VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)").run(principal.principal_id, principal.client_session_id, requestId, operation, attentionDigest(payload), missionId, JSON.stringify(result), now);
136:   }
137:   /** Completion is durable Session evidence even while the repository cannot be
138:    * observed. Keep its identity checks entirely inside the database transaction. */
139:   private databaseSource(mission: Pick<EngineeringMission, "scope_id" | "task_id" | "run_id" | "session_id" | "source_instance_id" | "repo_path">) {
140:     const sessions = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path); const session = sessions.getSession(mission.session_id);
141:     const scope = this.attention.getScope(mission.scope_id);
142:     if (!session || !scope) throw new EngineeringSourceFault("unavailable", "source_unavailable");
143:     if (session.task_id !== mission.task_id || session.run_id !== mission.run_id || scope.task_id !== mission.task_id
144:       || !session.native_session_id || engineeringSourceIdentity(session.session_id, session.native_session_id) !== mission.source_instance_id
145:       || scope.repo_path !== mission.repo_path) throw new EngineeringSourceFault("identity", "source_identity_changed");
146:     if (!this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM tasks t JOIN runs r ON r.task_id=t.task_id WHERE t.task_id=? AND r.run_id=? AND r.session_id=? AND r.agent_id=? AND t.tenant_id IS NULL AND r.tenant_id IS NULL").get(mission.task_id, mission.run_id, mission.session_id, session.agent_id)) throw new EngineeringSourceFault("identity", "source_scope_changed");
147:     return { session, snapshot: sessions.latestSnapshot(mission.session_id) };
148:   }
149:   private source(mission: Pick<EngineeringMission, "scope_id" | "task_id" | "run_id" | "session_id" | "source_instance_id" | "repo_path">) {
150:     const source = this.databaseSource(mission);
151:     let canonical: string;
152:     try { canonical = realpathSync(source.session.repo_path); }
153:     catch (error) {
154:       if (error && typeof error === "object" && "code" in error && ["ENOENT", "EIO", "ENOTDIR", "ESTALE", "ENODEV", "EBUSY", "EACCES", "EPERM"].includes(String(error.code))) throw new EngineeringSourceFault("unavailable", "repository_unavailable");
155:       throw error;
156:     }
157:     if (canonical !== mission.repo_path) throw new EngineeringSourceFault("identity", "repository_identity_changed");
158:     return source;
159:   }
160:   private sourceIssue(missionId: string, reason: string, category: string, now: number): void {
161:     if (!this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM engineering_events WHERE mission_id=? AND kind='source_observation_error' AND json_extract(payload_json,'$.reason')=?").get(missionId, reason)) this.event(missionId, "source_observation_error", null, { reason, category }, now);
162:   }
163:   private liveMessage(sessionId: string, messageId: string): boolean { return this.hostEvidence?.liveSend(sessionId) === messageId; }
164:   private ordinaryDecision(value: unknown): SessionDecision {
165:     const decision = SessionDecision.parse(value);
166:     const gate = decision.continuation?.gate_kind;
167:     if (gate === "risk_approval" || gate === "task_authorization") throw Error("signed_approval_required");
168:     if (gate === "phase_continue") throw Error("executor_not_qualified");
169:     return decision;
170:   }
171:   private ref(item: AttentionItem): EngineeringDecisionRef {
172:     return { kind: "session_decision", scope_id: item.scope_id, item_id: item.item_id, item_revision: item.revision, session_id: item.session_id,
173:       turn_id: item.turn_id, decision_id: item.decision_id, source_version: item.source_version, content_hash: item.content_hash };
174:   }
175:   private addWait(mission: EngineeringMission, snapshot: SessionSnapshot, now: number): void {
176:     if (!snapshot.turn_id) throw Error("source_unavailable");
177:     const decision = this.ordinaryDecision(snapshot.decision);
178:     const itemId = attentionDigest([mission.scope_id, mission.session_id, snapshot.turn_id, decision.decision_id]);
179:     const existing = this.db.prepare("SELECT mission_id FROM engineering_waits WHERE item_id=?").get(itemId) as { mission_id: string } | undefined;
180:     if (existing) {
181:       if (existing.mission_id !== mission.mission_id) throw Error("authority_already_adopted");
182:       return;
183:     }
184:     const item = this.attention.projectDecision(this.owner, { scopeId: mission.scope_id, sessionId: mission.session_id, turnId: snapshot.turn_id, sourceVersion: snapshot.version, decision, now });
185:     const prior = this.db.prepare("SELECT mission_id FROM engineering_waits WHERE item_id=?").get(item.item_id) as { mission_id: string } | undefined;
186:     if (prior && prior.mission_id !== mission.mission_id) throw Error("authority_already_adopted");
187:     if (prior) return;
188:     if ((this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM engineering_waits WHERE mission_id=?").get(mission.mission_id) as { n: number }).n >= 64) throw Error("budget_exhausted");
189:     const waitId = randomUUID();
190:     this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO engineering_waits VALUES(?,?,?,1,?,'open',NULL,NULL,NULL,0,?,?)").run(waitId, mission.mission_id, item.item_id, JSON.stringify(this.ref(item)), now, now);
191:     this.event(mission.mission_id, "decision_observed", null, { wait_id: waitId, decision_ref: this.ref(item) }, now);
192:   }
193:   private waits(missionId: string): WaitRow[] { return this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_waits WHERE mission_id=? ORDER BY created_at,wait_id LIMIT 100").all(missionId) as WaitRow[]; }
194:   private wait(missionId: string, waitId: string): WaitRow {
195:     const wait = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_waits WHERE mission_id=? AND wait_id=?").get(missionId, waitId) as WaitRow | undefined;
196:     if (!wait) throw Error("not_found"); return wait;
197:   }
198:   private setWait(wait: WaitRow, state: WaitRow["state"], now: number): void {
199:     if (wait.state !== state) this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_waits SET state=?,revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE wait_id=?").run(state, now, wait.wait_id);
200:   }
201:   adopt(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringAdoptRequest, now = Date.now()) {
202:     const input = EngineeringAdoptRequest.parse(value); this.clock(now);
203:     this.access(principal, input.scope_id, "read");
204:     return this.write(() => {
205:       const prior = this.request(principal, input.request_id, "adopt", input);
206:       if (prior) return { mission: this.readable(principal, prior.mission_id), replay: true };
207:       this.access(principal, input.scope_id, "write");
208:       const sessions = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path); const session = sessions.getSession(input.session_id);
209:       if (!session || !session.resume_supported || !session.native_session_id) throw Error("executor_not_qualified");
210:       const scope = this.attention.getScope(input.scope_id);
211:       if (!scope || scope.task_id !== input.task_id) throw Error("scope_mismatch");
212:       const mission: EngineeringMission = { mission_id: randomUUID(), scope_id: input.scope_id, task_id: input.task_id, run_id: input.run_id, session_id: input.session_id,
213:         repo_path: scope.repo_path, source_instance_id: engineeringSourceIdentity(session.session_id, session.native_session_id), source_generation: 1,
214:         owner_principal_id: principal.principal_id, owner_client_session_id: principal.client_session_id, goal: input.goal, completion_level: "observed_turn", state: "awaiting_decision", revision: 1,
215:         stop_intent_id: null, created_at: now, updated_at: now, observed_at: now, policy_digest: POLICY_DIGEST, observation_proof_json: null };
216:       const { snapshot } = this.source(mission);
217:       if (snapshot.state !== "awaiting_decision") throw Error("session_not_awaiting_decision");
218:       this.ordinaryDecision(snapshot.decision);
219:       if (this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM engineering_missions WHERE session_id=? AND state NOT IN ('completed','failed','cancel_requested','observation_ended')").get(input.session_id)) throw Error("source_already_adopted");
220:       if ((this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM engineering_missions").get() as { n: number }).n >= 1000) throw Error("budget_exhausted");
221:       this.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO engineering_missions VALUES(@mission_id,@scope_id,@task_id,@run_id,@session_id,@repo_path,@source_instance_id,@source_generation,@owner_principal_id,@owner_client_session_id,@goal,@completion_level,@state,@revision,@stop_intent_id,@created_at,@updated_at,@observed_at,@policy_digest,@observation_proof_json)`).run(mission);
222:       this.addWait(mission, snapshot, now); this.event(mission.mission_id, "session_adopted", principal.principal_id, { session_id: mission.session_id, source_instance_id: mission.source_instance_id, completion_level: "observed_turn" }, now);
223:       this.saveRequest(principal, input.request_id, "adopt", input, mission.mission_id, {}, now);
224:       return { mission, replay: false };
225:     });
226:   }
227:   observe(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringObserveRequest, now = Date.now()) {
228:     const input = EngineeringObserveRequest.parse(value); this.clock(now);
229:     const readable = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
230:     return this.attention.write(this.owner, () => {
231:       const sourceStatus = this.refresh(readable.mission_id, now);
232:       const mission = this.getMission(input.mission_id);
233:       let snapshot: SessionSnapshot | null = null;
234:       const sessions = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path);
235:       if (sourceStatus !== "unavailable" && sessions.getSession(mission.session_id)) snapshot = sessions.latestSnapshot(mission.session_id);
236:       const waits = this.waits(mission.mission_id).map((wait): EngineeringWait => {
237:         const { decision_ref_json, ...rest } = wait;
238:         const decision_ref = JSON.parse(decision_ref_json) as EngineeringDecisionRef;
239:         const item = this.attention.getItem(mission.scope_id, wait.item_id);
240:         if (!item) throw Error("source_unavailable");
241:         const result: EngineeringWait = { ...rest, decision_ref, decision: item.decision };
242:         if (!terminal.has(mission.state) && ["open", "deferred"].includes(wait.state) && principal.principal_id === mission.owner_principal_id && principal.write_scopes.includes(mission.scope_id)) {
243:           const token = randomBytes(32).toString("base64url"); const expires = now + 5 * 60_000;
244:           this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM engineering_interactions WHERE expires_at<?").run(now);
245:           this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM engineering_interactions WHERE mission_id=? AND rowid IN (SELECT rowid FROM engineering_interactions WHERE mission_id=? ORDER BY issued_at DESC,rowid DESC LIMIT -1 OFFSET 31)").run(mission.mission_id, mission.mission_id);
246:           this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO engineering_interactions VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)").run(attentionDigest(token), principal.principal_id, principal.client_session_id, mission.mission_id, mission.revision, wait.wait_id, item.item_id, decision_ref.item_revision, decision_ref.content_hash, now, expires);
247:           result.interaction_token = token; result.token_expires_at = expires;
248:         }
249:         return result;
250:       });
251:       const limit = input.limit ?? 50; const rows = this.db.prepare("SELECT seq,kind,actor,payload_json,created_at FROM engineering_events WHERE mission_id=? AND seq>? ORDER BY seq LIMIT ?").all(mission.mission_id, input.cursor ?? 0, limit + 1) as Array<{ seq: number; kind: string; actor: string | null; payload_json: string; created_at: number }>;
252:       const events = rows.slice(0, limit).map(({ payload_json, ...row }) => ({ ...row, payload: JSON.parse(payload_json) as unknown }));
253:       return { mission, waits, events, cursor: events.at(-1)?.seq ?? input.cursor ?? 0, has_more: rows.length > limit, source_status: sourceStatus,
254:         cost_evidence: { status: "unknown" as const, hard_cap_enforced: false as const, reason: "provider_usage_not_metered_by_adoption_pilot" as const },
255:         snapshot_digest: snapshot ? attentionDigest(snapshot) : null, source_version: snapshot?.version ?? null, turn_id: snapshot?.turn_id ?? null,
256:         observation_proof: mission.observation_proof_json ? JSON.parse(mission.observation_proof_json) as EngineeringObservationProof : null };
257:     });
258:   }
259:   decide(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringDecideRequest, now = Date.now()): EngineeringActionReceipt {
260:     const input = EngineeringDecideRequest.parse(value); this.clock(now); this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
261:     return this.write(() => {
262:       let mission = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
263:       const prior = this.request(principal, input.action_id, "decide", input);
264:       if (prior) return this.receipt(mission, input.action_id, true);
265:       this.steer(principal, mission); this.cas(mission, input.if_revision); this.controlBudget(mission.mission_id);
266:       const wait = this.wait(mission.mission_id, input.wait_id);
267:       if (!["open", "deferred"].includes(wait.state) || wait.action_id) throw Error("stale_decision");
268:       const ref = JSON.parse(wait.decision_ref_json) as EngineeringDecisionRef;
269:       if (attentionDigest(ref) !== attentionDigest(input.decision_ref)) throw Error("stale_decision");
270:       const token = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_interactions WHERE token_hash=?").get(attentionDigest(input.interaction_token)) as TokenRow | undefined;
271:       if (!token || token.principal_id !== principal.principal_id || token.client_session_id !== principal.client_session_id || token.mission_id !== mission.mission_id
272:         || token.mission_revision !== input.if_revision || token.wait_id !== wait.wait_id || token.item_id !== ref.item_id || token.item_revision !== ref.item_revision || token.content_hash !== ref.content_hash) throw Error("interaction_mismatch");
273:       if (now < token.issued_at || now > token.expires_at) throw Error("interaction_expired");
274:       const { session, snapshot } = this.source(mission);
275:       if (session.version !== ref.source_version || snapshot.turn_id !== ref.turn_id || attentionDigest(SessionDecision.parse(snapshot.decision)) !== ref.content_hash) throw Error("stale_decision");
276:       const decision = this.ordinaryDecision(snapshot.decision);
277:       let text: string;
278:       if (input.answer.kind === "choice") {
279:         const choiceId = input.answer.choice_id; const choice = decision.choices.find(c => c.choice_id === choiceId);
280:         if (!choice) throw Error("invalid_choice"); text = choice.label;
281:       } else {
282:         if (decision.choices.length > 0 || (decision.continuation && decision.continuation.gate_kind !== "external_input")) throw Error("explicit_choice_required");
283:         text = input.answer.text;
284:       }
285:       const reservation = new AttentionActions(this.attention, this.owner).reserve({ scope_id: mission.scope_id, item_id: ref.item_id, action_id: input.action_id, revision: ref.item_revision, content_hash: ref.content_hash, text, takeover: false }, now);
286:       if (reservation.replay || reservation.reservation_version !== ref.source_version + 1) throw Error("action_conflict");
287:       this.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO attention_dispatch_phases VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,1,'reserved',?,1,?,?,?,?,NULL,NULL,NULL,?,?)`).run(input.action_id, reservation.message_id, mission.mission_id, principal.principal_id, principal.client_session_id,
288:         mission.source_instance_id, ref.turn_id, ref.source_version, reservation.reservation_version, this.owner.generation, now, now);
289:       this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_waits SET state='answer_recorded',action_id=?,revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE wait_id=?").run(input.action_id, now, wait.wait_id);
290:       this.event(mission.mission_id, "answer_recorded", principal.principal_id, { action_id: input.action_id, message_id: reservation.message_id, wait_id: wait.wait_id, decision_ref: ref, client_session_id: principal.client_session_id, interaction_token_hash: attentionDigest(input.interaction_token), payload_digest: attentionDigest(input.answer) }, now);
291:       mission = this.bump(mission.mission_id, "answer_recorded", now);
292:       this.saveRequest(principal, input.action_id, "decide", input, mission.mission_id, { action_id: input.action_id }, now);
293:       return { mission, action_id: input.action_id, message_id: reservation.message_id, state: "reserved", replay: false, reservation_version: reservation.reservation_version };
294:     });
295:   }
296:   private receipt(mission: EngineeringMission, actionId: string, replay: boolean): EngineeringActionReceipt {
297:     const action = this.db.prepare("SELECT a.* FROM attention_actions a JOIN engineering_waits w ON w.action_id=a.action_id WHERE a.action_id=? AND w.mission_id=?").get(actionId, mission.mission_id) as ActionRow | undefined;
298:     if (!action) throw Error("not_found");
299:     const phase = this.getDispatch(action.message_id);
300:     const message = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(action.message_id);
301:     const resolved = this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?").get(action.message_id);
302:     const state = phase?.phase === "cancelled_before_dispatch"
303:       ? isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(this.db, action.message_id) ? "cancelled_before_dispatch" : "needs_reconciliation"
304:       : resolved ? "abandoned_unknown"
305:       : phase?.phase === "reserved" ? action.state === "reserved" && message?.state === "accepted" ? "reserved" : "needs_reconciliation"
306:       : message?.state === "applied" ? "applied"
307:       : phase?.phase === "dispatch_committed" && message?.state === "accepted" && this.liveMessage(mission.session_id, action.message_id) ? "in_flight" : "uncertain";
308:     return { mission, action_id: actionId, message_id: action.message_id, state, replay, reservation_version: phase?.reservation_version ?? 0 };
309:   }
310:   commitDispatch(actionId: string, principal: EngineeringPrincipal, now = Date.now()) {
311:     this.clock(now);
312:     return this.write(() => {
313:       const prepared = this.preflightDispatch(actionId, principal);
314:       const changed = this.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_dispatch_phases SET phase='dispatch_committed',committed_by_generation=?,updated_at=? WHERE action_id=? AND phase='reserved'").run(this.owner.generation, now, actionId);
315:       if (changed.changes !== 1) throw Error("dispatch_already_committed");
316:       const phase = this.getDispatch(prepared.message_id)!;
317:       this.event(phase.mission_id, "dispatch_committed", principal.principal_id, { action_id: actionId, message_id: prepared.message_id, daemon_generation: this.owner.generation }, now);
318:       const permit: EngineeringDispatchPermit = Object.freeze({ message_id: prepared.message_id, daemon_generation: this.owner.generation }); this.permits.add(permit);
319:       return { permit, ...prepared };
320:     });
321:   }
322:   /** Cheap read-only qualification before asynchronous admission. commitDispatch
323:    * repeats every predicate inside the owner-fenced FULL write transaction. */
324:   preflightDispatch(actionId: string, principal: EngineeringPrincipal) {
325:       this.attention.assertOwner(this.owner);
326:       if (this.db.pragma("synchronous", { simple: true }) !== 2) throw Error("durability_required");
327:       const phase = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_dispatch_phases WHERE action_id=?").get(actionId) as ReturnType<EngineeringStore["getDispatch"]>;
328:       if (!phase) throw Error("dispatch_gate_required");
329:       const mission = this.readable(principal, phase.mission_id); this.steer(principal, mission);
330:       if (phase.principal_id !== principal.principal_id || phase.client_session_id !== principal.client_session_id) throw Error("permission_denied");
331:       if (phase.phase !== "reserved") throw Error(phase.phase === "dispatch_committed" ? "dispatch_already_committed" : "cancelled_original_answer");
332:       if (terminal.has(mission.state) || mission.stop_intent_id) throw Error("cancelled_original_answer");
333:       if (mission.policy_digest !== POLICY_DIGEST) throw Error("policy_changed");
334:       const { session, snapshot } = this.source(mission);
335:       const action = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?").get(actionId) as ActionRow | undefined;
336:       const message = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(phase.message_id);
337:       const wait = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_waits WHERE action_id=? AND mission_id=?").get(actionId, mission.mission_id) as WaitRow | undefined;
338:       if (!action || action.state !== "reserved" || action.scope_id !== mission.scope_id || action.message_id !== phase.message_id || !message || message.state !== "accepted"
339:         || message.session_id !== mission.session_id || message.turn_id !== phase.original_turn_id || !wait || wait.state !== "answer_recorded" || wait.item_id !== action.item_id
340:         || phase.protocol_version !== 1 || phase.source_instance_id !== mission.source_instance_id || phase.source_generation !== mission.source_generation
341:         || session.version !== phase.reservation_version || snapshot.turn_id !== phase.original_turn_id || snapshot.state !== "awaiting_decision") throw Error("needs_reconciliation");
342:       if (this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?").get(phase.message_id)) throw Error("receipt_resolved");
343:       const ref = JSON.parse(wait.decision_ref_json) as EngineeringDecisionRef;
344:       const decision = this.ordinaryDecision(snapshot.decision);
345:       if (attentionDigest(decision) !== ref.content_hash || ref.source_version !== phase.source_version) throw Error("source_changed");
346:       const payload = JSON.parse(message.payload_json) as { text?: unknown };
347:       if (typeof payload.text !== "string" || action.payload_hash !== attentionDigest({ scope_id: mission.scope_id, item_id: ref.item_id, action_id: actionId, revision: ref.item_revision, content_hash: ref.content_hash, text: payload.text, takeover: false })) throw Error("payload_changed");
348:       return { session_id: mission.session_id, message_id: message.message_id, turn_id: phase.original_turn_id, reservation_version: phase.reservation_version, text: payload.text, action_id: actionId };
349:   }
350:   recordDispatchBlocked(actionId: string, reason: string, now = Date.now()): void {
351:     if (!/^[a-z_]{1,64}$/.test(reason)) throw Error("invalid_block_reason");
352:     this.write(() => {
353:       const phase = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_dispatch_phases WHERE action_id=?").get(actionId) as ReturnType<EngineeringStore["getDispatch"]>;
354:       if (!phase || phase.phase !== "reserved") return;
355:       const count = this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM engineering_events WHERE mission_id=? AND kind='dispatch_blocked' AND json_extract(payload_json,'$.action_id')=?").get(phase.mission_id, actionId) as { n: number };
356:       if (count.n >= 16 || this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM engineering_events WHERE mission_id=? AND kind='dispatch_blocked' AND json_extract(payload_json,'$.action_id')=? AND json_extract(payload_json,'$.reason')=?").get(phase.mission_id, actionId, reason)) return;
357:       this.event(phase.mission_id, "dispatch_blocked", null, { action_id: actionId, reason }, now);
358:     });
359:   }
360:   consumeDispatchPermit(permit: EngineeringDispatchPermit | undefined, messageId: string): void {
361:     this.attention.assertOwner(this.owner);
362:     if (!permit || permit.message_id !== messageId || permit.daemon_generation !== this.owner.generation || !this.permits.delete(permit)) throw Error("dispatch_gate_required");
363:   }
364:   defer(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringDeferRequest, now = Date.now()) {
365:     const input = EngineeringDeferRequest.parse(value); this.clock(now); this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
366:     return this.write(() => {
367:       const mission = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
368:       if (this.request(principal, input.request_id, "defer", input)) return { mission, replay: true };
369:       this.steer(principal, mission); this.cas(mission, input.if_revision); this.controlBudget(mission.mission_id);
370:       const { snapshot } = this.source(mission);
371:       const wait = this.wait(mission.mission_id, input.wait_id); const revisit = Date.parse(input.revisit_at);
372:       if (wait.revision !== input.wait_revision || !["open", "deferred"].includes(wait.state) || wait.action_id) throw Error("stale_decision");
373:       const ref = JSON.parse(wait.decision_ref_json) as EngineeringDecisionRef;
374:       if (snapshot.turn_id !== ref.turn_id || snapshot.version !== ref.source_version || attentionDigest(SessionDecision.parse(snapshot.decision)) !== ref.content_hash) throw Error("stale_decision");
375:       if (revisit <= now || revisit > now + 7 * 86400_000) throw Error("invalid_revisit_at");
376:       this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_waits SET state='deferred',revision=revision+1,revisit_at=?,reason=?,reminder_sent=0,updated_at=? WHERE wait_id=?").run(revisit, input.reason, now, wait.wait_id);
377:       this.event(mission.mission_id, "decision_deferred", principal.principal_id, { wait_id: wait.wait_id, revisit_at: input.revisit_at, reason: input.reason }, now);
378:       const updated = this.bump(mission.mission_id, mission.state, now); this.saveRequest(principal, input.request_id, "defer", input, mission.mission_id, {}, now);
379:       return { mission: updated, replay: false };
380:     });
381:   }
382:   cancel(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringCancelRequest, now = Date.now()): EngineeringStopResult { return this.end(principal, EngineeringCancelRequest.parse(value), false, now); }
383:   endObservation(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringEndObservationRequest, now = Date.now()): EngineeringStopResult { return this.end(principal, EngineeringEndObservationRequest.parse(value), true, now); }
384:   private end(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, input: EngineeringCancelRequest, observationOnly: boolean, now: number): EngineeringStopResult {
385:     this.clock(now); this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
386:     return this.write(() => {
387:       const mission = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id); const operation = observationOnly ? "endObservation" : "cancel";
388:       const prior = this.request(principal, input.request_id, operation, input);
389:       if (prior) return { ...(JSON.parse(prior.response_json) as EngineeringStopResult), mission, replay: true };
390:       const isOwner = mission.owner_principal_id === principal.principal_id;
391:       if (observationOnly) {
392:         if (!isOwner) { this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "operator"); this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "stop"); }
393:       } else {
394:         this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "stop");
395:         if (!isOwner) this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "operator");
396:       }
397:       if (mission.revision !== input.if_revision) throw Error("version_conflict");
398:       const phases = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_dispatch_phases WHERE mission_id=?").all(mission.mission_id) as NonNullable<ReturnType<EngineeringStore["getDispatch"]>>[];
399:       const committedIds = phases.filter(phase => phase.phase === "dispatch_committed"
400:         && new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(phase.message_id)?.state !== "applied").map(phase => phase.message_id);
401:       if (mission.state === "completed" || mission.state === "failed") throw Error("mission_terminal");
402:       if (terminal.has(mission.state) && (observationOnly || committedIds.length === 0)) throw Error("mission_terminal");
403:       // Retain the first durable intent. A bounded later attempt may request stop
404:       // only for this Mission's unresolved committed messages, never a later send.
405:       const stopAttempts = (this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM engineering_requests WHERE mission_id=? AND operation='cancel'").get(mission.mission_id) as { n: number }).n;
406:       if (!observationOnly && stopAttempts >= 8) throw Error("stop_attempt_budget_exhausted");
407:       const intent = mission.stop_intent_id ?? randomUUID();
408:       const kind = observationOnly ? "observation_ended" : stopAttempts > 0 ? "stop_retry_requested" : "stop_requested";
409:       const eventSeq = this.event(mission.mission_id, kind, principal.principal_id, { stop_intent_id: intent, request_id: input.request_id, run_id: mission.run_id, session_id: mission.session_id, action_ids: phases.map(p => p.action_id), owner_generation: this.owner.generation, reason: input.reason ?? null }, now);
410:       if (mission.stop_intent_id === null) this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_missions SET stop_intent_id=? WHERE mission_id=?").run(intent, mission.mission_id);
411:       for (const phase of phases) {
412:         if (phase.phase !== "reserved") continue;
413:         const action = this.db.prepare("SELECT state,message_id FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?").get(phase.action_id) as { state: string; message_id: string } | undefined;
414:         const message = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(phase.message_id);
415:         if (!action || action.state !== "reserved" || action.message_id !== phase.message_id || message?.state !== "accepted" || message.session_id !== mission.session_id
416:           || this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?").get(phase.message_id)) continue;
417:         const proof = { version: 1, prior_phase: "reserved", action_id: phase.action_id, message_id: phase.message_id, stop_intent_id: intent, event_seq: eventSeq, owner_generation: this.owner.generation };
418:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_dispatch_phases SET phase='cancelled_before_dispatch',stop_intent_id=?,proof_json=?,updated_at=? WHERE action_id=? AND phase='reserved'").run(intent, JSON.stringify(proof), now, phase.action_id);
419:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_actions SET state='cancelled_before_dispatch',updated_at=? WHERE action_id=?").run(now, phase.action_id);
420:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_items SET state='answer_cancelled',revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE item_id IN (SELECT item_id FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?)").run(now, phase.action_id);
421:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_waits SET state='cancelled_before_dispatch',revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE action_id=?").run(now, phase.action_id);
422:       }
423:       const updated = this.bump(mission.mission_id, observationOnly ? "observation_ended" : "cancel_requested", now);
424:       const result: EngineeringStopResult = { mission: updated, replay: false, stop_intent_id: intent, run_id: mission.run_id, session_id: mission.session_id, action_ids: phases.map(p => p.action_id), committed_message_ids: committedIds };
425:       this.saveRequest(principal, input.request_id, operation, input, mission.mission_id, result, now); return result;
426:     });
427:   }
428:   recordStopReceipt(missionId: string, intentId: string, status: "stop_requested" | "stop_unknown", evidence: unknown, now = Date.now()): void {
429:     this.write(() => {
430:       const mission = this.getMission(missionId);
431:       if (mission.stop_intent_id !== intentId) throw Error("stop_intent_changed");
432:       const kind = status === "stop_unknown" ? "stop_unknown" : "stop_request_acknowledged";
433:       const payload = { stop_intent_id: intentId, evidence };
434:       if (!this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM engineering_events WHERE mission_id=? AND kind=? AND payload_json=?").get(missionId, kind, JSON.stringify(payload))) this.event(missionId, kind, null, payload, now);
435:     });
436:   }
437:   reconcile(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringReconcileRequest, now = Date.now()) {
438:     const input = EngineeringReconcileRequest.parse(value); this.clock(now); const initial = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
439:     if (input.kind === "query_action") return this.attention.write(this.owner, () => this.receipt(initial, input.action_id, true));
440:     return this.write(() => {
441:       const mission = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
442:       if (this.request(principal, input.request_id, "dispose_wait", input)) return { mission, replay: true };
443:       this.steer(principal, mission); this.cas(mission, input.if_revision); this.controlBudget(mission.mission_id);
444:       const wait = this.wait(mission.mission_id, input.wait_id);
445:       if (wait.revision !== input.wait_revision || wait.action_id || cleared.has(wait.state)) throw Error("receipt_requires_reconciliation");
446:       if (this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_actions WHERE item_id=?").get(wait.item_id)) throw Error("receipt_requires_reconciliation");
447:       const { snapshot } = this.source(mission);
448:       if (snapshot.version !== input.observed_source_version || snapshot.turn_id !== input.observed_turn_id || attentionDigest(snapshot) !== input.observed_snapshot_digest) throw Error("source_changed");
449:       const item = this.attention.getItem(mission.scope_id, wait.item_id); if (!item) throw Error("source_unavailable"); this.ordinaryDecision(item.decision);
450:       this.setWait(wait, "superseded", now);
451:       this.event(mission.mission_id, "steering_recorded", principal.principal_id, { wait_id: wait.wait_id, disposition: input.disposition, observed_snapshot_digest: input.observed_snapshot_digest, reason: input.reason, delivery_verified: false }, now);
452:       const updated = this.bump(mission.mission_id, "observing", now); this.saveRequest(principal, input.request_id, "dispose_wait", input, mission.mission_id, {}, now);
453:       return { mission: updated, replay: false };
454:     });
455:   }
456:   /** Must be called from the same transaction that marks the original message
457:    * applied and writes its new Session turn. Do not access the repository here:
458:    * refresh observes it later before projecting Mission completion. */
459:   recordCompletion(messageId: string, completedTurnId: string, sessionVersion: number, now = Date.now()): void {
460:     if (!this.db.inTransaction) throw Error("completion_transaction_required");
461:     this.attention.assertOwner(this.owner);
462:     const phase = this.getDispatch(messageId); if (!phase) return;
463:     if (phase.phase !== "dispatch_committed") throw Error("dispatch_inconsistent");
464:     const mission = this.getMission(phase.mission_id); const { snapshot } = this.databaseSource(mission);
465:     if (phase.source_instance_id !== mission.source_instance_id || phase.source_generation !== mission.source_generation) throw Error("source_changed");
466:     const message = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(messageId);
467:     if (message?.state !== "applied" || message.session_id !== mission.session_id || snapshot.turn_id !== completedTurnId || snapshot.version !== sessionVersion || sessionVersion !== phase.reservation_version + 1) throw Error("completion_mismatch");
468:     const proof: EngineeringObservationProof = { kind: "observed_turn", action_id: phase.action_id, message_id: messageId, source_instance_id: phase.source_instance_id, source_generation: phase.source_generation, completed_turn_id: completedTurnId, session_version: sessionVersion, output_sha256: attentionDigest(snapshot.assistant_text) };
469:     const prior = this.db.prepare("SELECT proof_json FROM engineering_completions WHERE action_id=?").get(phase.action_id) as { proof_json: string } | undefined;
470:     if (prior && prior.proof_json !== JSON.stringify(proof)) throw Error("completion_mismatch");
471:     if (!prior) {
472:       this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO engineering_completions VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?)").run(phase.action_id, messageId, mission.mission_id, completedTurnId, sessionVersion, JSON.stringify(proof), now);
473:       this.event(mission.mission_id, "answer_applied", null, proof, now);
474:     }
475:     this.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_actions SET state='applied',updated_at=? WHERE action_id=? AND message_id=? AND state<>'applied'").run(now, phase.action_id, messageId);
476:     this.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_items SET state='answer_applied',revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE item_id IN (SELECT item_id FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=? AND message_id=?) AND state<>'answer_applied'").run(now, phase.action_id, messageId);
477:   }
478:   /** Deterministic bounded projection; empty polls neither invoke a model nor
479:    * increment the Mission's intent revision. */
480:   refresh(missionId: string, now = Date.now()): SourceStatus {
481:     this.clock(now);
482:     return this.attention.write(this.owner, () => {
483:       let mission = this.getMission(missionId); if (terminal.has(mission.state)) return "not_observing";
484:       let snapshot: SessionSnapshot;
485:       try { snapshot = this.source(mission).snapshot; }
486:       catch (error) {
487:         if (!(error instanceof EngineeringSourceFault)) throw error;
488:         this.sourceIssue(missionId, error.reason, error.category, now);
489:         if (error.category === "unavailable") return "unavailable";
490:         for (const wait of this.waits(missionId)) if (!cleared.has(wait.state)) this.setWait(wait, "needs_reconciliation", now);
491:         if (mission.state !== "needs_reconciliation") this.bump(missionId, "needs_reconciliation", now); return "identity_mismatch";
492:       }
493:       let uncertain = false;
494:       for (const wait of this.waits(missionId)) {
495:         if (cleared.has(wait.state)) continue;
496:         if (wait.state === "needs_reconciliation") { uncertain = true; continue; }
497:         if (wait.state === "deferred" && wait.revisit_at !== null && now >= wait.revisit_at && now >= wait.updated_at && wait.reminder_sent === 0) {
498:           this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_waits SET reminder_sent=1 WHERE wait_id=? AND reminder_sent=0").run(wait.wait_id);
499:           this.event(missionId, "decision_reminder_due", null, { wait_id: wait.wait_id, revisit_at: wait.revisit_at, reminder_budget: 1 }, now);
500:         }
501:         if (wait.action_id) {
502:           const action = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?").get(wait.action_id) as ActionRow | undefined;
503:           if (!action) { this.setWait(wait, "needs_reconciliation", now); uncertain = true; continue; }
504:           const phase = this.getDispatch(action.message_id); const completion = this.db.prepare("SELECT proof_json FROM engineering_completions WHERE action_id=?").get(wait.action_id) as { proof_json: string } | undefined;
505:           if (completion && new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(action.message_id)?.state === "applied") { this.setWait(wait, "cleared", now); continue; }
506:           if (phase?.phase === "cancelled_before_dispatch" && isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(this.db, action.message_id)) { this.setWait(wait, "cancelled_before_dispatch", now); continue; }
507:           if (phase?.phase === "dispatch_committed" && new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(action.message_id)?.state === "accepted" && this.liveMessage(mission.session_id, action.message_id)) {
508:             this.setWait(wait, "answer_recorded", now); continue;
509:           }
510:           const receipt = new AttentionActions(this.attention, this.owner).reconcile(action, now);
511:           if (receipt.state === "reserved") continue;
512:           this.setWait(wait, "delivery_unknown", now); uncertain = true;
513:         } else {
514:           const ref = JSON.parse(wait.decision_ref_json) as EngineeringDecisionRef;
515:           const parsed = SessionDecision.safeParse(snapshot.decision);
516:           if (snapshot.turn_id !== ref.turn_id || snapshot.version !== ref.source_version || !parsed.success || attentionDigest(parsed.data) !== ref.content_hash) {
517:             this.setWait(wait, "needs_reconciliation", now); uncertain = true; this.sourceIssue(missionId, "decision_source_changed", "decision", now);
518:           }
519:         }
520:       }
521:       if (snapshot.state === "awaiting_decision" && snapshot.decision) {
522:         try { this.addWait(mission, snapshot, now); }
523:         catch (error) {
524:           const reason = error instanceof Error && error.name === "ZodError" ? "invalid_decision" : error instanceof Error ? error.message : "";
525:           if (!["signed_approval_required", "executor_not_qualified", "decision_content_conflict", "authority_already_adopted", "budget_exhausted", "source_unavailable", "invalid_decision"].includes(reason)) throw error;
526:           uncertain = true; this.sourceIssue(missionId, reason, "decision", now);
527:         }
528:       }
529:       const waits = this.waits(missionId); const unclosed = waits.some(wait => !cleared.has(wait.state));
530:       const completion = this.db.prepare("SELECT proof_json FROM engineering_completions WHERE mission_id=? AND completed_turn_id=? AND session_version=?").get(missionId, snapshot.turn_id, snapshot.version) as { proof_json: string } | undefined;
531:       if (!uncertain && !unclosed && !mission.stop_intent_id && completion && snapshot.decision === null && ["idle", "completed"].includes(snapshot.state)) {
532:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_missions SET observation_proof_json=? WHERE mission_id=?").run(completion.proof_json, missionId);
533:         mission = this.bump(missionId, "completed", now); this.event(missionId, "observation_completed", null, JSON.parse(completion.proof_json), now);
534:       } else {
535:         const state = uncertain ? "needs_reconciliation" : waits.some(w => ["open", "deferred"].includes(w.state)) ? "awaiting_decision" : waits.some(w => w.state === "answer_recorded") ? "answer_recorded" : "observing";
536:         if (mission.state !== state) this.bump(missionId, state, now);
537:       }
538:       // FULL durability applies to the shared connection. Empty one-second
539:       // watchdog ticks must not issue a persistent heartbeat every time.
540:       if (now >= mission.observed_at + 60_000) this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_missions SET observed_at=? WHERE mission_id=?").run(now, missionId);
541:       return "available";
542:     });
543:   }
544: }

184: function finish(f: ReturnType<typeof setup>, store: EngineeringStore, messageId: string) {
185:   f.db.transaction(() => {
186:     new SessionRepository(f.db, f.path).completeInboundTurn({ message_id: messageId, expected_version: 2, applied_at: 2, turn: { turn_id: "done", session_id: "s", ordinal: 2, state: "completed", assistant_text: "Kept", decision: null, created_at: 2, updated_at: 2 } });

190: it("persists a real completed turn during a repository outage and projects completion only after observation recovers", () => {
191:   const f = setup(); const a = f.store.decide(f.principal, f.answer, 1002); f.store.commitDispatch(a.action_id, f.principal, 1003);
192:   const moved = `${f.root}-temporarily-unavailable`;
193:   renameSync(f.root, moved);
194:   try {
195:     finish(f, f.store, a.message_id);
196:     const sessions = new SessionRepository(f.db, f.path);
197:     expect(sessions.getMessage(a.message_id)?.state).toBe("applied");
198:     expect(sessions.latestSnapshot("s")).toMatchObject({ turn_id: "done", version: 3, state: "completed", assistant_text: "Kept" });
199:     expect(f.db.prepare("SELECT state FROM attention_actions").get()).toEqual({ state: "applied" });
200:     expect(f.db.prepare("SELECT state FROM attention_items").get()).toEqual({ state: "answer_applied" });
201:     expect(f.db.prepare("SELECT message_id,completed_turn_id,session_version FROM engineering_completions").get()).toEqual({ message_id: a.message_id, completed_turn_id: "done", session_version: 3 });
202:     expect(f.store.getMission(a.mission.mission_id).state).toBe("answer_recorded");
203:     // The completion transaction does not attempt repository observation.
204:     expect(f.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM engineering_events WHERE kind='source_observation_error'").get()).toEqual({ n: 0 });

206:     expect(f.store.getMission(a.mission.mission_id).state).toBe("answer_recorded");
207:     expect(f.db.prepare("SELECT state FROM engineering_waits").get()).toEqual({ state: "answer_recorded" });
208:   } finally { renameSync(moved, f.root); }
209:   expect(f.store.refresh(a.mission.mission_id, 9002)).toBe("available");
210:   expect(f.store.getMission(a.mission.mission_id).state).toBe("completed");

===== FILE packages/core/src/state/engineering-dispatch.ts sha256=36b03aea0604875d67c41ac2c2c1e807d0e4cc7d2dc593ac9b4c2a24ae469abd =====
1: import type Database from "better-sqlite3";

3: export interface EngineeringDispatchPhase {
4:   action_id: string; message_id: string; mission_id: string; principal_id: string; client_session_id: string;
5:   protocol_version: 1; phase: "reserved" | "dispatch_committed" | "cancelled_before_dispatch";
6:   source_instance_id: string; source_generation: number; original_turn_id: string;
7:   source_version: number; reservation_version: number; reserved_by_generation: number;
8:   committed_by_generation: number | null; stop_intent_id: string | null; proof_json: string | null;
9:   created_at: number; updated_at: number;
10: }
11: export function getEngineeringDispatchPhase(db: Database.Database, messageId: string): EngineeringDispatchPhase | undefined {
12:   return db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_dispatch_phases WHERE message_id=?").get(messageId) as EngineeringDispatchPhase | undefined;
13: }

15: /** A phase label alone cannot release the uncertain-message guard. The original
16:  * message/action, immutable cancellation transition, and durable intent agree. */
17: export function isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(db: Database.Database, messageId: string): boolean {
18:   const phase = getEngineeringDispatchPhase(db, messageId);
19:   if (!phase || phase.phase !== "cancelled_before_dispatch") return false;
20:   const bound = db.prepare(`SELECT m.state,a.state action_state,a.action_id,a.message_id,i.session_id,i.turn_id,e.kind,e.payload_json
21:     FROM attention_actions a JOIN session_messages m ON m.message_id=a.message_id
22:     JOIN attention_items i ON i.item_id=a.item_id AND i.scope_id=a.scope_id
23:     JOIN engineering_missions x ON x.mission_id=? AND x.session_id=m.session_id AND x.scope_id=a.scope_id
24:     JOIN engineering_events e ON e.mission_id=x.mission_id
25:     WHERE a.action_id=? AND a.message_id=? AND i.session_id=m.session_id AND i.turn_id=m.turn_id AND e.seq=?`).get(
26:       phase.mission_id, phase.action_id, messageId, readProofSequence(phase.proof_json),
27:     ) as { state: string; action_state: string; action_id: string; message_id: string; session_id: string; turn_id: string; kind: string; payload_json: string } | undefined;
28:   if (!bound || bound.state === "applied" || bound.action_state !== "cancelled_before_dispatch" || bound.turn_id !== phase.original_turn_id || !["stop_requested", "observation_ended"].includes(bound.kind)
29:     || db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?").get(messageId)) return false;
30:   try {
31:     const proof = JSON.parse(phase.proof_json ?? "null") as Record<string, unknown> | null;
32:     const event = JSON.parse(bound.payload_json) as Record<string, unknown>;
33:     return proof?.["version"] === 1 && proof["prior_phase"] === "reserved"
34:       && proof["action_id"] === phase.action_id && proof["message_id"] === messageId
35:       && proof["stop_intent_id"] === phase.stop_intent_id && event["stop_intent_id"] === phase.stop_intent_id
36:       && event["session_id"] === bound.session_id && Array.isArray(event["action_ids"]) && event["action_ids"].includes(phase.action_id)
37:       && event["owner_generation"] === proof["owner_generation"] && Number.isSafeInteger(proof["owner_generation"]) && Number(proof["owner_generation"]) > 0;
38:   } catch { return false; }
39: }
40: function readProofSequence(value: string | null): number {
41:   try { const v = JSON.parse(value ?? "null")?.event_seq; return Number.isSafeInteger(v) ? v : -1; } catch { return -1; }
42: }

===== FILE packages/core/src/state/db.ts sha256=bda761c2999f90292d546b6b871ad83567b851cc605767d6d6cc12417f183fee =====
1: // SQLite connection management.
2: //
3: // Decisions locked in plan v0.6:
4: //   - better-sqlite3 (synchronous, single connection per daemon process)
5: //   - WAL mode for concurrent reader/writer safety
6: //   - synchronous=NORMAL (WAL-safe, faster than FULL)
7: //   - 64 MiB page cache
8: //   - foreign_keys ON (so ON DELETE CASCADE works for runs/events on task delete)
9: //
10: // CRITICAL GAP G1 (plan v0.6 §8.3): disk-full must never cause a silent task
11: // write loss. Every write goes through preflightDiskSpace(), which throws
12: // DiskFullError before any INSERT/UPDATE can corrupt or stall a transaction.
13: 
14: import Database from "better-sqlite3";
15: import { statfsSync } from "node:fs";
16: import { dirname } from "node:path";
17: import { CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION, MIGRATIONS } from "./migrations.js";
18: 
19: /** Default safety threshold for disk-full preflight (100 MiB). */
20: export const DEFAULT_MIN_FREE_BYTES = 100 * 1024 * 1024;
21: 
22: /** Cache size in -kibibyte units. negative = KiB (per SQLite docs). 64 MiB. */
23: const PAGE_CACHE_KIB = -65536;
24: 
25: export class DiskFullError extends Error {
26:   readonly path: string;
27:   readonly freeBytes: number;
28:   readonly minFreeBytes: number;
29:   constructor(path: string, freeBytes: number, minFreeBytes: number) {
30:     super(
31:       `Disk full preflight failed for ${path}: ${freeBytes} bytes free, ` +
32:       `need ≥${minFreeBytes}. Refusing write so Hermes can be notified.`,
33:     );
34:     this.name = "DiskFullError";
35:     this.path = path;
36:     this.freeBytes = freeBytes;
37:     this.minFreeBytes = minFreeBytes;
38:   }
39: }
40: 
41: export interface OpenDbOptions {
42:   /** Absolute path to the SQLite file (parent dir must exist). */
43:   path: string;
44:   /** When false, opens read-only. */
45:   readwrite?: boolean;
46:   /** Override the disk-full safety threshold (bytes). */
47:   minFreeBytes?: number;
48: }
49: 
50: /**
51:  * Run a free-space check against the filesystem holding `path`. Throws
52:  * DiskFullError if below threshold; otherwise returns free byte count.
53:  * Skips the check (returns Infinity) if statfsSync is unsupported on the
54:  * platform — better to allow writes than to crash a daemon on a kernel quirk.
55:  */
56: export function preflightDiskSpace(
57:   path: string,
58:   minFreeBytes: number = DEFAULT_MIN_FREE_BYTES,
59: ): number {
60:   let stats: { bsize: number; bavail: number };
61:   try {
62:     stats = statfsSync(dirname(path));
63:   } catch {
64:     return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
65:   }
66:   const free = Number(stats.bsize) * Number(stats.bavail);
67:   if (free < minFreeBytes) throw new DiskFullError(path, free, minFreeBytes);
68:   return free;
69: }
70: 
71: /**
72:  * Open the osstack state database, apply pragmas, and run pending migrations.
73:  * Caller owns the lifetime — pair every openDb() with a db.close() in shutdown.
74:  */
75: export function openDb(opts: OpenDbOptions): Database.Database {
76:   const { path, readwrite = true, minFreeBytes = DEFAULT_MIN_FREE_BYTES } = opts;
77:   if (readwrite) preflightDiskSpace(path, minFreeBytes);
78: 
79:   const db = new Database(path, { readonly: !readwrite });
80: 
81:   try {
82:     // Refuse an unsupported schema before changing journal mode or running DDL,
83:     // including read-only inspection. A failed open never escapes with a live handle.
84:     assertSupportedVersion(db.pragma("user_version", { simple: true }) as number);
85:     assertMigrationLineage(db);
86:     // Pragmas are applied per-connection in SQLite. Apply on every open.
87:     if (readwrite) db.pragma("journal_mode = WAL");
88:     db.pragma("synchronous = NORMAL");
89:     db.pragma(`cache_size = ${PAGE_CACHE_KIB}`);
90:     db.pragma("foreign_keys = ON");
91:     db.pragma("busy_timeout = 5000");
92: 
93:     if (readwrite) runMigrations(db);
94:     return db;
95:   } catch (error) {
96:     db.close();
97:     throw error;
98:   }
99: }
100: 
101: // This schema is table-additive. Derive expected table introductions once;
102: // a future DROP/RENAME migration must also update this lineage contract.
103: const tableIntroductions = MIGRATIONS.flatMap(migration =>
104:   [...migration.sql.matchAll(/^\s*CREATE\s+TABLE(?:\s+IF\s+NOT\s+EXISTS)?\s+([a-zA-Z_]\w*)\s*\(/gm)]
105:     .map(match => ({ name: match[1]!, version: migration.id })));
106: 
107: /** Reject pure or mixed unreleased Graph schemas before application pragmas or
108:  * DDL. SQLite may itself checkpoint an abandoned WAL on close; logical ledger
109:  * content and user_version must remain intact for the original binary. */
110: function assertMigrationLineage(db: Database.Database): void {
111:   const current = db.pragma("user_version", { simple: true }) as number;
112:   const actual = new Set((db.prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'").all() as { name: string }[]).map(row => row.name));
113:   // Experimental Graph or Attention tables must never be migrated as released main.
114:   const experimentalTable = (name: string) => name.startsWith("graph_") || name.startsWith("attention_") || name.startsWith("engineering_") || name === "daemon_owner";
115:   if (current < 25 && ![...actual].some(experimentalTable)) return;
116:   const expected = new Set(tableIntroductions.filter(table => table.version <= current).map(table => table.name));
117:   if ([...expected].some(name => !actual.has(name))
118:     || [...actual].some(name => experimentalTable(name) && !expected.has(name))) {
119:     throw new Error("GRAPH_EXPERIMENTAL_SCHEMA_CONFLICT: preserve this home and reconcile with its original build; do not reset user_version or reuse it for the integrated runtime");
120:   }
121: }
122: 
123: function assertSupportedVersion(current: number): void {
124:   if (current > CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
125:     throw new Error(`Migration state inconsistent: user_version=${current}, expected=${CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION}`);
126:   }
127: }
128: 
129: /**
130:  * Apply pending migrations idempotently. `user_version` PRAGMA tracks progress.
131:  * The version pragma guarantees forward-only application. Many migrations
132:  * contain non-idempotent CREATE/ALTER statements; never reset user_version.
133:  */
134: export function runMigrations(db: Database.Database): void {
135:   const currentRow = db.pragma("user_version", { simple: true }) as number;
136:   let current = Number(currentRow);
137:   assertSupportedVersion(current);
138:   assertMigrationLineage(db);
139:   for (const m of MIGRATIONS) {
140:     if (m.id <= current) continue;
141:     db.exec("BEGIN");
142:     try {
143:       db.exec(m.sql);
144:       db.pragma(`user_version = ${m.id}`);
145:       db.exec("COMMIT");
146:       current = m.id;
147:     } catch (err) {
148:       db.exec("ROLLBACK");
149:       throw err;
150:     }
151:   }
152:   if (current !== CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
153:     throw new Error(
154:       `Migration state inconsistent: user_version=${current}, expected=${CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION}`,
155:     );
156:   }
157: }
158: 
159: /**
160:  * Run a function inside a transaction with preflight. Use for any write path
161:  * called from the daemon hot loop. Wraps better-sqlite3's transaction() so
162:  * disk-space errors fire BEFORE BEGIN, not mid-transaction.
163:  */
164: export function withWrite<T>(
165:   db: Database.Database,
166:   path: string,
167:   fn: (db: Database.Database) => T,
168:   minFreeBytes: number = DEFAULT_MIN_FREE_BYTES,
169: ): T {
170:   preflightDiskSpace(path, minFreeBytes);
171:   const tx = db.transaction(fn);
172:   return tx(db);
173: }

79: function parseSession(row: RawSessionRow): AgentSessionRowType {

83: function canonicalJson(value: unknown): string {
84:   if (value === null || typeof value !== "object") return JSON.stringify(value);
85:   if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(canonicalJson).join(",")}]`;
86:   const entries = Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
87:     .sort(([left], [right]) => left.localeCompare(right));
88:   return `{${entries.map(([key, item]) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonicalJson(item)}`).join(",")}}`;
89: }

108:         parsed.resume_supported ? 1 : 0, parsed.version, parsed.updated_at,
109:       );

111:   }
112: 
113:   getSession(sessionId: string): AgentSessionRowType | undefined {
114:     const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
115:       .get(sessionId) as RawSessionRow | undefined;
116:     return row ? parseSession(row) : undefined;
117:   }
118: 
119:   recordNativeSession(
120:     sessionId: string,
121:     nativeSessionId: string,
122:     expectedVersion: number,
123:     updatedAt: number,
124:   ): AgentSessionRowType {
125:     return this.updateSession(
126:       sessionId,
127:       expectedVersion,
128:       updatedAt,
129:       "native_session_id = ?, state = 'idle'",
130:       [nativeSessionId],
131:     );
132:   }
133: 
134:   setState(
135:     sessionId: string,
136:     state: AgentSessionStateType,
137:     expectedVersion: number,
138:     updatedAt: number,
139:   ): AgentSessionRowType {
140:     AgentSessionState.parse(state);
141:     return this.updateSession(sessionId, expectedVersion, updatedAt, "state = ?", [state]);
142:   }
143: 
144:   appendTurn(input: SessionTurnInput): void {
145:     const { decision, ...stored } = input;
146:     const row = SessionTurnRow.parse({
147:       ...stored,
148:       decision_json: decision === null ? null : canonicalJson(decision),
149:     });
150:     withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
151:       db.prepare(`
152:         INSERT INTO session_turns(
153:           turn_id,session_id,ordinal,state,assistant_text,decision_json,created_at,updated_at
154:         ) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
155:       `).run(
156:         row.turn_id, row.session_id, row.ordinal, row.state, row.assistant_text,
157:         row.decision_json, row.created_at, row.updated_at,
158:       );
159:     });
160:   }
161: 
162:   applyInbound(
163:     input: SessionMessageInput,
164:     expectedVersion: number,
165:     appliedAt: number,
166:   ): ApplyMessageResult {
167:     const { payload, ...stored } = input;
168:     const payloadJson = canonicalJson(payload);
169:     const row = SessionMessageRow.parse({
170:       ...stored,
171:       payload_json: payloadJson,
172:       applied_at: input.applied_at ?? null,
173:     });
174: 
175:     return withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
176:       const existing = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM session_messages WHERE message_id = ?")
177:         .get(row.message_id) as SessionMessageRowType | undefined;
178:       if (existing) {
179:         if (
180:           existing.session_id !== row.session_id
181:           || existing.turn_id !== row.turn_id
182:           || existing.kind !== row.kind
183:           || existing.payload_json !== payloadJson
184:         ) {
185:           throw new SessionMessageIdConflict(row.message_id);
186:         }
187:         const session = this.readSession(db, row.session_id);
188:         return { outcome: "duplicate", session_version: session.version };
189:       }
190: 
191:       const result = db.prepare(`
192:         UPDATE agent_sessions
193:         SET version = version + 1, updated_at = ?
194:         WHERE session_id = ? AND version = ?
195:       `).run(appliedAt, row.session_id, expectedVersion);
196:       if (result.changes !== 1) {
197:         const actual = db.prepare("SELECT version FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
198:           .get(row.session_id) as { version: number } | undefined;
199:         throw new SessionVersionConflict(row.session_id, expectedVersion, actual?.version ?? null);
200:       }
201: 
202:       db.prepare(`
203:         INSERT INTO session_messages(
204:           message_id,session_id,turn_id,direction,kind,payload_json,state,created_at,applied_at
205:         ) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
206:       `).run(
207:         row.message_id, row.session_id, row.turn_id, row.direction, row.kind,
208:         payloadJson, row.state, row.created_at, row.applied_at,
209:       );
210:       return { outcome: "applied", session_version: expectedVersion + 1 };
211:     });
212:   }
213: 
214:   markMessageApplied(messageId: string, appliedAt: number): void {
215:     withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
216:       const result = db.prepare(`
217:         UPDATE session_messages
218:         SET state = 'applied', applied_at = ?
219:         WHERE message_id = ? AND state = 'accepted'
220:       `).run(appliedAt, messageId);
221:       if (result.changes !== 1) {
222:         throw new Error(`message ${messageId} is missing or is not accepted`);
223:       }
224:     });
225:   }
226: 
227:   markMessageFailed(messageId: string): void {
228:     withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
229:       const result = db.prepare(`
230:         UPDATE session_messages
231:         SET state = 'failed', applied_at = NULL
232:         WHERE message_id = ? AND state = 'accepted'
233:       `).run(messageId);
234:       if (result.changes !== 1) {
235:         throw new Error(`message ${messageId} is missing or is not accepted`);
236:       }
237:     });
238:   }
239: 
240:   retryFailedMessage(messageId: string): void {
241:     withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
242:       const result = db.prepare(`
243:         UPDATE session_messages
244:         SET state = 'accepted', applied_at = NULL
245:         WHERE message_id = ? AND state = 'failed'
246:       `).run(messageId);
247:       if (result.changes !== 1) {
248:         throw new Error(`message ${messageId} is missing or is not failed`);
249:       }
250:     });
251:   }
252: 
253:   completeInboundTurn(input: CompleteInboundTurnInput): AgentSessionRowType {
254:     AgentSessionState.parse(input.turn.state);
255:     const { decision, ...storedTurn } = input.turn;
256:     const turn = SessionTurnRow.parse({
257:       ...storedTurn,
258:       decision_json: decision === null ? null : canonicalJson(decision),
259:     });
260: 
261:     return withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {

264:       `).get(input.message_id) as {
265:         session_id: string;
266:         state: SessionMessageRowType["state"];
267:       } | undefined;
268:       if (
269:         message === undefined
270:         || message.session_id !== turn.session_id
271:         || message.state !== "accepted"

273:         throw new Error(
274:           `message ${input.message_id} is missing, belongs to another session, or is not accepted`,
275:         );
276:       }
277: 
278:       const updated = db.prepare(`
279:         UPDATE agent_sessions
280:         SET state = ?, version = version + 1, updated_at = ?
281:         WHERE session_id = ? AND version = ?
282:       `).run(
283:         turn.state,
284:         input.applied_at,
285:         turn.session_id,
286:         input.expected_version,
287:       );
288:       if (updated.changes !== 1) {
289:         const actual = db.prepare("SELECT version FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
290:           .get(turn.session_id) as { version: number } | undefined;
291:         throw new SessionVersionConflict(
292:           turn.session_id,
293:           input.expected_version,
294:           actual?.version ?? null,
295:         );
296:       }
297: 
298:       db.prepare(`
299:         INSERT INTO session_turns(
300:           turn_id,session_id,ordinal,state,assistant_text,decision_json,created_at,updated_at
301:         ) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
302:       `).run(
303:         turn.turn_id,
304:         turn.session_id,
305:         turn.ordinal,
306:         turn.state,
307:         turn.assistant_text,
308:         turn.decision_json,
309:         turn.created_at,
310:         turn.updated_at,
311:       );
312: 
313:       const applied = db.prepare(`
314:         UPDATE session_messages
315:         SET state = 'applied', applied_at = ?
316:         WHERE message_id = ? AND state = 'accepted'
317:       `).run(input.applied_at, input.message_id);
318:       if (applied.changes !== 1) {
319:         throw new Error(`message ${input.message_id} could not be marked applied`);
320:       }
321:       return this.readSession(db, turn.session_id);
322:     });
323:   }
324: 
325:   getMessage(messageId: string): SessionMessageRowType | undefined {
326:     const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM session_messages WHERE message_id = ?")
327:       .get(messageId) as SessionMessageRowType | undefined;
328:     return row ? SessionMessageRow.parse(row) : undefined;
329:   }
330: 
331:   latestSnapshot(sessionId: string): SessionSnapshot {
332:     const session = this.readSession(this.db, sessionId);
333:     const turn = this.db.prepare(`
334:       SELECT * FROM session_turns WHERE session_id = ? ORDER BY ordinal DESC LIMIT 1
335:     `).get(sessionId) as SessionTurnRowType | undefined;
336:     const parsedTurn = turn ? SessionTurnRow.parse(turn) : undefined;
337:     return {
338:       session_id: session.session_id,
339:       task_id: session.task_id,
340:       run_id: session.run_id,
341:       agent_id: session.agent_id,
342:       runtime_id: session.agent_id,
343:       state: session.state,
344:       version: session.version,
345:       turn_id: parsedTurn?.turn_id ?? null,
346:       assistant_text: parsedTurn?.assistant_text ?? "",
347:       decision: parsedTurn?.decision_json ? JSON.parse(parsedTurn.decision_json) : null,
348:       updated_at: session.updated_at,
349:     };
350:   }
351: 
352:   listSessions(filter: SessionFilter = {}): AgentSessionRowType[] {
353:     const where: string[] = [];
354:     const values: Array<string | number> = [];
355:     if (filter.agent_id !== undefined) {
356:       where.push("agent_id = ?");
357:       values.push(filter.agent_id);
358:     }
359:     if (filter.state !== undefined) {
360:       AgentSessionState.parse(filter.state);
361:       where.push("state = ?");
362:       values.push(filter.state);
363:     }
364:     if (filter.task_id !== undefined) {
365:       where.push("task_id = ?");
366:       values.push(filter.task_id);
367:     }
368:     const limit = filter.limit ?? 200;
369:     if (!Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 200) {
370:       throw new Error("session list limit must be an integer between 1 and 200");
371:     }
372:     values.push(limit);
373:     const sql = `SELECT * FROM agent_sessions${where.length > 0 ? ` WHERE ${where.join(" AND ")}` : ""} ORDER BY updated_at DESC, session_id LIMIT ?`;
374:     return (this.db.prepare(sql).all(...values) as RawSessionRow[]).map(parseSession);
375:   }
376: 
377:   private updateSession(
378:     sessionId: string,
379:     expectedVersion: number,
380:     updatedAt: number,
381:     assignment: string,
382:     values: unknown[],
383:   ): AgentSessionRowType {
384:     return withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
385:       const result = db.prepare(`
386:         UPDATE agent_sessions
387:         SET ${assignment}, version = version + 1, updated_at = ?
388:         WHERE session_id = ? AND version = ?
389:       `).run(...values, updatedAt, sessionId, expectedVersion);
390:       if (result.changes !== 1) {
391:         const actual = db.prepare("SELECT version FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
392:           .get(sessionId) as { version: number } | undefined;
393:         throw new SessionVersionConflict(sessionId, expectedVersion, actual?.version ?? null);
394:       }
395:       return this.readSession(db, sessionId);
396:     });
397:   }
398: 
399:   private readSession(db: Database.Database, sessionId: string): AgentSessionRowType {
400:     const row = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
401:       .get(sessionId) as RawSessionRow | undefined;
402:     if (!row) throw new SessionVersionConflict(sessionId, 0, null);
403:     return parseSession(row);
404:   }
405: }

13: /** Common human reservation, serialized with collaborator writes in the same DB. */
14: export function reserveSessionInstruction(
15:   db: Database.Database,
16:   path: string,
17:   message: SessionMessageInput,
18:   version: number,
19:   now: number,
20:   takeover = false,
21:   takeoverGrantId?: string
22: ) {
23:   return withWrite(db, path, () => {
24:     const sessions = new SessionRepository(db, path);
25:     if (sessions.getMessage(message.message_id))
26:       return sessions.applyInbound(message, version, now);
27:     if ((db.prepare("SELECT p.message_id FROM attention_dispatch_phases p JOIN session_messages m ON m.message_id=p.message_id WHERE m.session_id=? 28:     if ((db.prepare("SELECT message_id FROM session_messages WHERE session_id=? 30:     if (db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_dispatch_phases p JOIN session_messages m ON m.message_id=p.message_id WHERE m.session_id=? AND p.phase='cancelled_before_dispatch' AND m.state='applied'").get(message.session_id)) throw new Error("dispatch_inconsistent");
31:     if (

35:         )
36:         .get(message.session_id)
37:     )

40:       .prepare(
