51:     const raw = snapshot.decision as Record<string, unknown> | null;
52:     const legacy =
53:       raw !== null &&
54:       typeof raw === "object" &&
55:       !Array.isArray(raw) &&
56:       Object.keys(raw).every((k) => ["question", "choices"].includes(k)) &&
57:       Array.isArray(raw["choices"]) &&
58:       raw["choices"].every((v) => typeof v === "string") &&
59:       (raw["question"] === undefined || typeof raw["question"] === "string");
60:     if (snapshot.decision !== null && !decision.success && !legacy)
61:       throw new Error("invalid_decision");
62: 
63:     if (
64:       decision.success &&
65:       ["risk_approval", "task_authorization"].includes(

68:     )

70:     if (grant)

72:         "UPDATE session_continuation_grants SET status='blocked',reason='human_takeover',updated_at=? WHERE grant_id=?"
73:       ).run(now, grant.grant_id);

77: export interface AttentionAnswer {
78:   scope_id: string;
79:   item_id: string;
80:   action_id: string;
81:   revision: number;
82:   content_hash: string;
83:   text: string;
84:   takeover: boolean;
85:   takeover_grant_id?: string;
86: }
87: interface Action {
88:   action_id: string;
89:   scope_id: string;
90:   item_id: string;
91:   payload_hash: string;
92:   message_id: string;
93:   state: string;
94:   created_at: number;
95:   updated_at: number;
96: }
97: export class AttentionActions {
98:   constructor(readonly store: AttentionStore, readonly owner: AttentionOwner) {}
99:   reserve(input: AttentionAnswer, now = Date.now()) {
100:     if (
101:       !input.action_id ||
102:       input.action_id.length > 256 ||
103:       !input.text.trim() ||
104:       input.text.length > 16000
105:     )
106:       throw new Error("invalid_answer");
107:     return this.store.write(this.owner, () => {
108:       const db = this.store.db;
109:       const digest = attentionDigest(input);
110:       const prior = db
111:         .prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?")
112:         .get(input.action_id) as Action | undefined;
113:       if (prior) {
114:         if (prior.payload_hash !== digest)
115:           throw new Error("action_id_conflict");
116:         return {
117:           ...this.reconcile(prior, now),
118:           replay: true,
119:           reservation_version: 0,
120:         };
121:       }
122:       const item = this.store.getItem(input.scope_id, input.item_id);
123:       const scope = this.store.getScope(input.scope_id);
124:       if (!item || !scope) throw new Error("not_found");
125:       if (
126:         item.state !== "open" ||
127:         item.revision !== input.revision ||
128:         item.content_hash !== input.content_hash
129:       )
130:         throw new Error("stale_item");
131:       if (
132:         db
133:           .prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_actions WHERE item_id=?")
134:           .get(item.item_id)
135:       )
136:         throw new Error("action_already_reserved");
137:       const repo = new SessionRepository(db, this.store.path);
138:       const source = repo.getSession(item.session_id);
139:       const latest = repo.latestSnapshot(item.session_id);
140:       if (source && source.state !== "awaiting_decision")
141:         throw new Error("session_not_awaiting_decision");
142:       if (
143:         !source ||
144:         source.task_id !== scope.task_id ||
145:         realpathSync(source.repo_path) !== scope.repo_path ||
146:         latest.turn_id !== item.turn_id ||
147:         source.version !== item.source_version ||
148:         attentionDigest(SessionDecision.parse(latest.decision)) !==
149:           item.content_hash
150:       )
151:         throw new Error("source_changed");
152:       if (
153:         !db
154:           .prepare(
155:             "SELECT 1 FROM tasks t JOIN runs r ON r.task_id=t.task_id WHERE t.task_id=? AND r.run_id=? AND t.tenant_id IS NULL AND r.tenant_id IS NULL"
156:           )
157:           .get(scope.task_id, source.run_id)
158:       )
159:         throw new Error("source_changed");
160:       const messageId = `attention-${randomUUID()}`;
161:       const reservation = reserveSessionInstruction(
162:         db,
163:         this.store.path,
164:         {
165:           message_id: messageId,
166:           session_id: item.session_id,
167:           turn_id: item.turn_id,
168:           direction: "inbound",
169:           kind: "instruction.send",
170:           payload: { text: input.text },
171:           state: "accepted",
172:           created_at: now / 1000,
173:         },
174:         source.version,
175:         now / 1000,
176:         input.takeover,
177:         input.takeover_grant_id
178:       );
179:       db.prepare(
180:         "INSERT INTO attention_actions VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,'reserved',?,?)"
181:       ).run(
182:         input.action_id,
183:         input.scope_id,
184:         input.item_id,
185:         digest,
186:         messageId,
187:         now,
188:         now
189:       );
190:       db.prepare(
191:         "UPDATE attention_items SET state='answer_reserved',revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE item_id=?"
192:       ).run(now, item.item_id);
193:       return {
194:         action_id: input.action_id,
195:         scope_id: input.scope_id,
196:         item_id: input.item_id,
197:         payload_hash: digest,
198:         message_id: messageId,
199:         state: "reserved",
200:         created_at: now,
201:         updated_at: now,
202:         replay: false,
203:         reservation_version: reservation.session_version,
204:       };
205:     });
206:   }
207:   replay(input: AttentionAnswer, applyingMessageId?: string) {
208:     const prior = this.store.db
209:       .prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?")
210:       .get(input.action_id) as Action | undefined;
211:     if (!prior) return undefined;
212:     if (prior.payload_hash !== attentionDigest(input))
213:       throw new Error("action_id_conflict");
214:     if (getEngineeringDispatchPhase(this.store.db, prior.message_id)?.phase === "cancelled_before_dispatch") return { ...this.reconcile(prior), replay: true };
215:     if (prior.message_id === applyingMessageId)
216:       return { ...prior, state: "in_flight", replay: true };
217:     return { ...this.reconcile(prior), replay: true };
218:   }
219:   abandon(
220:     input: {
221:       scope_id: string;
222:       session_id: string;
223:       message_id: string;
224:       expected_version: number;
225:       reason: string;
226:       evidence_sha256: string;
227:     },
228:     now = Date.now()
229:   ) {
230:     if (
231:       !input.reason ||
232:       input.reason.length > 1000 ||
233:       !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(input.evidence_sha256)
234:     )
235:       throw new Error("resolution_evidence_required");
236:     return this.store.write(this.owner, () => {
237:       const db = this.store.db;
238:       const session = new SessionRepository(db, this.store.path).getSession(
239:         input.session_id
240:       );
241:       if (!session || session.version !== input.expected_version)
242:         throw new Error("version_conflict");
243:       const scope = this.store.getScope(input.scope_id);
244:       if (
245:         !scope ||
246:         scope.task_id !== session.task_id ||
247:         scope.repo_path !== realpathSync(session.repo_path)
248:       )
249:         throw new Error("scope_mismatch");
250:       const message = db
251:         .prepare(
252:           "SELECT state FROM session_messages WHERE message_id=? AND session_id=? AND direction='inbound'"
253:         )
254:         .get(input.message_id, input.session_id) as
255:         | { state: string }
256:         | undefined;
257:       const phase = getEngineeringDispatchPhase(db, input.message_id);
258:       if (phase?.phase === "reserved") throw new Error("not_dispatched_use_cancel");
259:       if (phase?.phase === "cancelled_before_dispatch") {
260:         if (!isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(db, input.message_id)) throw new Error("dispatch_inconsistent");
261:         return { disposition: "cancelled_before_dispatch", message_id: input.message_id, replayed: true, execution_outcome: "not_dispatched", next_action: "The original answer is cancelled. It cannot be sent again." };
262:       }
263:       if (!message || !["accepted", "failed"].includes(message.state))
264:         throw new Error("receipt_not_uncertain");
265:       if (
266:         db
267:           .prepare(
268:             "SELECT 1 FROM session_continuation_attempts a JOIN session_continuation_grants g ON a.grant_id=g.grant_id WHERE g.session_id=? AND a.status IN ('checking','reserved')"
269:           )
270:           .get(input.session_id)
271:       )
272:         throw new Error("continuation_in_flight");
273:       const prior = db
274:         .prepare(
275:           "SELECT * FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?"
276:         )
277:         .get(input.message_id) as
278:         | { reason: string; evidence_sha256: string }
279:         | undefined;
280:       if (
281:         prior &&
282:         (prior.reason !== input.reason ||
283:           prior.evidence_sha256 !== input.evidence_sha256)
284:       )
285:         throw new Error("resolution_conflict");
286:       db.prepare(
287:         "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO attention_receipt_resolutions VALUES(?,?,?,'abandoned_unknown',?,?,?)"
288:       ).run(
289:         input.message_id,
290:         input.session_id,
291:         input.expected_version,
292:         input.reason,
293:         input.evidence_sha256,
294:         now
295:       );
296:       db.prepare(
297:         "UPDATE attention_actions SET state='abandoned_unknown',updated_at=? WHERE message_id=?"
298:       ).run(now, input.message_id);
299:       db.prepare(
300:         "UPDATE attention_items SET state='answer_abandoned',revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE item_id IN (SELECT item_id FROM attention_actions WHERE message_id=?) AND state<>'answer_abandoned'"
301:       ).run(now, input.message_id);
302:       return {
303:         disposition: "abandoned_unknown",
304:         message_id: input.message_id,
305:         replayed: false,
306:         execution_outcome: "unknown",
307:         next_action:
308:           "New manual instruction only; original message is never resent",
309:       };
310:     });
311:   }
312:   recover(): void {
313:     const rows = this.store.db
314:       .prepare(
315:         "SELECT * FROM attention_actions WHERE state IN ('reserved','uncertain')"
316:       )
317:       .all() as Action[];
318:     for (const row of rows) this.reconcile(row);
319:   }
320:   reconcile(action: Action, now = Date.now()): Action {
321:     return this.store.write(this.owner, () => {
322:       const phase = getEngineeringDispatchPhase(this.store.db, action.message_id);
323:       if (phase?.phase === "cancelled_before_dispatch") {
324:         if (!isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(this.store.db, action.message_id)) return { ...action, state: "needs_reconciliation" };
325:         return { ...action, state: "cancelled_before_dispatch" };
326:       }
327:       if (
328:         this.store.db
329:           .prepare(
330:             "SELECT 1 FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?"
331:           )
332:           .get(action.message_id)
333:       )
334:         return { ...action, state: "abandoned_unknown" };
335:       const receipt = this.store.db
336:         .prepare("SELECT state FROM session_messages WHERE message_id=?")
337:         .get(action.message_id) as { state: string } | undefined;
338:       if (phase?.phase === "reserved") {
339:         return { ...action, state: action.state === "reserved" && receipt?.state === "accepted" ? "reserved" : "needs_reconciliation" };
340:       }
341:       const state =
342:         receipt?.state === "applied"
343:           ? "applied"
344:           : receipt?.state === "failed"
345:           ? "uncertain"
346:           : "uncertain";
347:       this.store.db
348:         .prepare(
349:           "UPDATE attention_actions SET state=?,updated_at=? WHERE action_id=? AND state<>?"
350:         )
351:         .run(state, now, action.action_id, state);
352:       this.store.db
353:         .prepare(
354:           "UPDATE attention_items SET state=?,revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE item_id=? AND state<>?"
355:         )
356:         .run(
357:           state === "applied" ? "answer_applied" : "answer_uncertain",
358:           now,
359:           action.item_id,
360:           state === "applied" ? "answer_applied" : "answer_uncertain"
361:         );
362:       return { ...action, state, updated_at: state === action.state ? action.updated_at : now };
363:     });
364:   }
365: }

1: import { isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch, type EngineeringStore, type SessionRepository } from "@osstack/core";
2: import { EngineeringRpcRequests } from "@osstack/protocol";
3: import type { EngineeringAccess } from "./engineering-access.js";
4: import type { ExecutionLease } from "./governed-execution.js";

6: type Session = NonNullable<ReturnType<SessionRepository["getSession"]>>;
7: type Dispatch = ReturnType<EngineeringStore["commitDispatch"]>;
8: type Admission = { lease: ExecutionLease; controller: AbortController; release(): Promise<void> };
9: export interface EngineeringHostPorts {
10:   getSession(id: string): Session | undefined;
11:   qualified(session: Session): boolean;

19: /** No second sender/outbox. This host only drives first dispatch of the existing receipt. */
20: export class EngineeringHost {
21:   private readonly pending = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
22:   private closed = false;
23:   private readonly blocked = new Map<string, { attempts: number; nextAt: number }>();
24:   private timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | undefined;
25:   private cursor: { created_at: number; action_id: string } | undefined;
26:   private missionCursor: { created_at: number; mission_id: string } | undefined;
27:   constructor(readonly store: EngineeringStore, readonly access: EngineeringAccess, private readonly ports: EngineeringHostPorts) {}
28: 
29:   async handle(method: string, params: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
30:     if (this.closed) throw new Error("engineering_unavailable");
31:     if (!(method in EngineeringRpcRequests)) throw new Error("unknown_method");
32:     const schema = EngineeringRpcRequests[method as keyof typeof EngineeringRpcRequests];
33:     const parsed = schema.safeParse(params);
34:     if (!parsed.success) throw new Error("bad_request");
35:     const { credential, ...input } = parsed.data;
36:     const actor = this.access.authenticate(credential);
37:     // Reparse under the concrete schema below: the wire union cannot infer the method correlation.
38:     if (method === "engineering.adopt") {
39:       if (this.ports.draining()) throw new Error("draining");
40:       const p = EngineeringRpcRequests["engineering.adopt"].parse(params);
41:       if (!actor.read_scopes.includes(p.scope_id)) throw new Error("permission_denied");
42:       const scope = this.store.attention.getScope(p.scope_id);
43:       const session = this.ports.getSession(p.session_id);
44:       if (!scope || scope.task_id !== p.task_id || !session || session.task_id !== scope.task_id || session.run_id !== p.run_id) throw new Error("permission_denied");
45:       if (!this.ports.qualified(session)) throw new Error("executor_not_qualified");
46:       this.access.assertOutsideRepository(session.repo_path);
47:       const { credential: _credential, ...request } = p;
48:       return this.store.adopt(actor, request);
49:     }
50:     if (!("mission_id" in input)) throw new Error("bad_request");
51:     const mission = this.store.readableMission(actor, input.mission_id);
52:     this.access.assertOutsideCanonicalRepository(mission.repo_path);
53:     switch (method) {
54:       case "engineering.observe": {
55:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
56:         return this.store.observe(actor, p);
57:       }
58:       case "engineering.decide": {
59:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
60:         // Read-only receipt replay is allowed while drained or at capacity. The
61:         // domain still verifies actor/scope and the complete immutable payload.
62:         const replay = this.store.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM engineering_requests WHERE principal_id=? AND client_session_id=? AND request_id=? AND operation='decide'").get(actor.principal_id, actor.client_session_id, p.action_id);
63:         if (!replay && this.ports.draining()) throw new Error("draining");
64:         if (!replay && this.pending.size >= 16) throw new Error("engineering_busy");
65:         const result = this.store.decide(actor, p);
66:         if (!result.replay) {
67:           const phase = this.store.getDispatch(result.message_id);
68:           if (phase) this.schedule(phase.action_id);
69:         }
70:         return result;
71:       }
72:       case "engineering.defer": {
73:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
74:         return this.store.defer(actor, p);
75:       }
76:       case "engineering.cancel": {
77:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
78:         const result = this.store.cancel(actor, p);
79:         if (!result.replay) {
80:           const cancelledMessages = this.cancelledAdmissions(result.mission.mission_id, result.action_ids);
81:           const requested = this.ports.stop({ runId: result.run_id, sessionId: result.session_id,
82:             messageIds: [...result.committed_message_ids, ...cancelledMessages] });
83:           const provenUnstarted = result.committed_message_ids.length === 0 && cancelledMessages.length > 0
84:             && cancelledMessages.length === result.action_ids.length;
85:           // A queue reservation proven never dispatched needs no speculative
86:           // Worker-stop receipt. Preserve the exact non-dispatch evidence.
87:           if (!provenUnstarted) this.store.recordStopReceipt(result.mission.mission_id, result.stop_intent_id, "stop_unknown", {
88:             abort_requested: requested,
89:             reason: requested ? "abort is a request; containment exit is not proven" : "no live invocation in this daemon; prior worker exit is unknown",
90:           });
91:         }
92:         return result;
93:       }
94:       case "engineering.endObservation": {
95:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
96:         const result = this.store.endObservation(actor, p);
97:         if (!result.replay) {
98:           // Ending observation may release only an original queue reservation
99:           // proven never dispatched. It must not stop a committed Worker.
100:           const messages = this.cancelledAdmissions(result.mission.mission_id, result.action_ids);
101:           if (messages.length) this.ports.stop({ runId: result.run_id, sessionId: result.session_id, messageIds: messages });
102:         }
103:         return result;
104:       }
105:       case "engineering.reconcile": {
106:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
107:         return this.store.reconcile(actor, p);
108:       }
109:       default: throw new Error("unknown_method");
110:     }
111:   }
112: 
113:   private cancelledAdmissions(missionId: string, actionIds: readonly string[]): string[] {
114:     const messages: string[] = [];
115:     for (const actionId of actionIds) {
116:       const phase = this.store.db.prepare("SELECT message_id FROM attention_dispatch_phases WHERE mission_id=? AND action_id=? AND phase='cancelled_before_dispatch'").get(missionId, actionId) as { message_id: string } | undefined;
117:       if (phase && isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(this.store.db, phase.message_id)) messages.push(phase.message_id);
118:     }
119:     return messages;
120:   }
121:   /** No model polling. Bounded pages let a denied old action coexist with later work. */
122:   start(): void {
123:     if (this.closed || this.timer) return;
124:     this.tick();
125:     this.timer = setInterval(() => this.tick(), 1000);
126:     this.timer.unref();
127:   }
128:   private tick(): void {
129:     if (this.closed || this.ports.draining()) return;
130:     this.recover();
131:     try {
132:       const rows = this.store.listActiveMissions(16, this.missionCursor);
133:       for (const row of rows) {
134:         try { this.store.refresh(row.mission_id); } catch { /* Retain original source obligations. */ }
135:       }
136:       const last = rows.at(-1);
137:       this.missionCursor = rows.length === 16 && last ? { created_at: last.created_at, mission_id: last.mission_id } : undefined;
138:     } catch { /* Fencing failures cannot become observer or dispatch authority. */ }
139:   }
140:   /** Only persisted reserved phases are eligible, never committed/legacy. */
141:   recover(): void {
142:     if (this.closed || this.ports.draining()) return;
143:     try {
144:       const rows = this.store.listReserved(16, this.cursor);
145:       for (const row of rows) this.schedule(row.action_id);
146:       const last = rows.at(-1);
147:       this.cursor = rows.length === 16 && last ? { created_at: last.created_at, action_id: last.action_id } : undefined;
148:     } catch { /* A stale owner or unavailable database cannot authorize dispatch. */ }
149:   }
150:   private schedule(actionId: string): void {
151:     if (this.closed || this.ports.draining() || this.pending.has(actionId) || this.pending.size >= 16 || (this.blocked.get(actionId)?.nextAt ?? 0) > Date.now()) return;
152:     // Let the durable received response become observable before expensive admission.
153:     const pending = new Promise<void>(resolve => setImmediate(resolve))
154:       .then(() => this.dispatch(actionId))
155:       .catch(error => {
156:         const reason = error instanceof Error && /^[a-z_]{1,64}$/.test(error.message) ? error.message : "admission_denied";
157:         const attempts = Math.min((this.blocked.get(actionId)?.attempts ?? 0) + 1, 7);
158:         this.blocked.set(actionId, { attempts, nextAt: Date.now() + Math.min(60_000, 1000 * 2 ** (attempts - 1)) });
159:         // The durable store deduplicates each reason. A denied old owner must
160:         // neither spin on writer admission nor disappear behind a swallowed error.
161:         try { this.store.recordDispatchBlocked(actionId, reason); } catch { /* A stale owner cannot write diagnostics. */ }
162:       })
163:       .finally(() => this.pending.delete(actionId));
164:     this.pending.set(actionId, pending);
165:   }
166:   private async dispatch(actionId: string): Promise<void> {
167:     if (this.closed || this.ports.draining()) return;
168:     const row = this.store.getReserved(actionId);
169:     if (!row) { this.blocked.delete(actionId); return; }
170:     // Cheap read-only rejection comes before any health probe, semaphore or
171:     // repository writer lock. The final post-admission checks still run below.
172:     const preliminaryActor = this.access.refresh(row.owner_principal_id, row.client_session_id);
173:     this.store.preflightDispatch(actionId, preliminaryActor);
174:     const session = this.ports.getSession(row.session_id);
175:     if (!session || !this.ports.qualified(session)) throw new Error("executor_not_qualified");
176:     if (this.ports.active(session.session_id)) throw new Error("session_busy");
177:     this.access.assertOutsideRepository(session.repo_path);
178:     const admission = await this.ports.acquire(session, row.message_id);
179:     let handedOff = false;
180:     try {
181:       if (this.closed || this.ports.draining() || admission.controller.signal.aborted) return;
182:       // The latest authority is read after asynchronous admission, immediately before synchronous CAS.
183:       const actor = this.access.refresh(row.owner_principal_id, row.client_session_id);
184:       this.access.assertOutsideRepository(session.repo_path);
185:       const dispatch = this.store.commitDispatch(actionId, actor);
186:       this.blocked.delete(actionId);
187:       handedOff = true;
188:       // A pilot reply turn has a host-enforced five-minute wall-clock budget.
189:       // Abort requests cancellation; the existing helper retains admission if
190:       // actual adapter exit remains unproved.
191:       const deadline = setTimeout(() => admission.controller.abort(), 300_000);
192:       deadline.unref();
193:       try { await this.ports.execute(dispatch, session, admission); }
194:       finally { clearTimeout(deadline); }
195:     } finally { if (!handedOff) await admission.release(); }
196:   }
197:   async close(): Promise<void> {
198:     this.closed = true;
199:     if (this.timer) clearInterval(this.timer);
200:     this.timer = undefined;
201:     await Promise.allSettled(this.pending.values());
202:   }
203: }

13: class FixtureAdapter implements SessionCapableAdapter {
14:   readonly id = "engineering-fixture";
15:   readonly display_name = "Trusted in-process fixture";
16:   readonly cost_tier = "low" as const;
17:   readonly resumed: AgentSessionInput[] = [];
18:   mode: "complete" | "await_abort" | "await_release" | "unknown" = "complete";
19:   releaseTurn: (()=>void) | undefined;
20:   readonly aborted = new Set<string>();
21:   capabilities() { return []; }
22:   async isAvailable() { return {available:true,status:"available" as const,last_checked_at:Date.now()/1000}; }
23:   async *invoke(): AsyncIterable<HermesEvent> { yield* []; }
24:   sessionCapabilities() { return {resume:true,snapshot:true}; }
25:   async *startSession(): AsyncIterable<AgentSessionEvent> { yield* []; throw new Error("adoption must not start a worker"); }
26:   async confirmStopped() { return {stopped:true as const,evidence:"fixture iterator owns no process"}; }
27:   async *resumeSession(input: AgentSessionInput, signal: AbortSignal): AsyncIterable<AgentSessionEvent> {
28:     this.resumed.push(input);
29:     yield {type:"native_session.started",native_session_id:input.native_session_id};
30:     if (this.mode === "await_abort") {
31:       if (!signal.aborted) await new Promise<void>(resolve => signal.addEventListener("abort",()=>{this.aborted.add(input.message_id);resolve();},{once:true}));
32:       yield {type:"session.terminal",state:"cancelled"};return;
33:     }
34:     if (this.mode === "await_release") await new Promise<void>(resolve=>{this.releaseTurn=resolve;signal.addEventListener("abort",()=>resolve(),{once:true});});
35:     if (this.mode === "unknown") throw new Error("lost response after invocation");
36:     yield {type:"assistant.message",message_id:"reply",text:"Reply turn complete"};
37:     yield {type:"session.terminal",state:"completed"};
38:   }
39: }
40: type Response = {ok:boolean;result?:Record<string,unknown>;error?:{code:string}};
41: let daemon: DaemonHandles | undefined;
42: let root: string | undefined;
43: let socket: string;
44: let authPath: string;
45: let repo: string;
46: let scope: string;
47: let adapter: FixtureAdapter;
48: let gate: Promise<void> | undefined;
49: let releaseGate: (()=>void) | undefined;
50: let admissionChecks=0;
51: const credential="engineering-owner-secret-".repeat(2);
52: const operatorCredential="engineering-operator-secret-".repeat(2);
53: const writerCredential="engineering-other-writer-".repeat(2);
54: function rows(write=true, ownerStop=true) {
55:   return [
56:     {credential_sha256:hash(credential),principal_id:"owner",client_session_id:"owner-ui",read_scopes:[scope],write_scopes:write?[scope]:[],stop_scopes:ownerStop?[scope]:[],operator_scopes:[]},
57:     {credential_sha256:hash(operatorCredential),principal_id:"operator",client_session_id:"ops-ui",read_scopes:[scope],write_scopes:[],stop_scopes:[scope],operator_scopes:[scope]},
58:     {credential_sha256:hash(writerCredential),principal_id:"writer",client_session_id:"writer-ui",read_scopes:[scope],write_scopes:[scope],stop_scopes:[],operator_scopes:[]},
59:   ];
60: }
61: function hash(value:string) {return createHash("sha256").update(value).digest("hex");}
62: function authority(value: unknown[]) {writeFileSync(authPath,JSON.stringify({version:1,principals:value}),{mode:0o600});}
63: function rpc(method:string,params:unknown={}):Promise<Response> {
64:   return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
65:     const sock=connect(socket);const decoder=new FrameDecoder();
66:     const timer=setTimeout(()=>{sock.destroy();reject(new Error("RPC timeout"));},5000);
67:     sock.on("connect",()=>sock.write(encodeFrame({id:1,method,params})));
68:     sock.on("error",error=>{clearTimeout(timer);reject(error);});
69:     sock.on("data",chunk=>{const frames=decoder.push(chunk);if(frames.length){clearTimeout(timer);sock.end();resolve(frames[0] as Response);}});
70:   });
71: }
72: async function until<T>(read:()=>T|Promise<T>,predicate:(value:T)=>boolean):Promise<T> {
73:   const deadline=Date.now()+5000;
74:   while(true){const value=await read();if(predicate(value))return value;if(Date.now()>deadline)throw new Error(`condition timeout: ${JSON.stringify(value)}`);await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,15));}
75: }
76: async function boot() {
77:   daemon=await startDaemon({socketPath:socket,adapters:[adapter],workspacePath:repo,autonomyHealth:async()=>{admissionChecks++;if(gate)await gate;return healthyAutonomyHealth();}});
78: }

88:   db.prepare("INSERT INTO session_turns(turn_id,session_id,ordinal,state,decision_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES('turn','session',1,'awaiting_decision',?,1,1)").run(JSON.stringify({decision_id:"decision",question:"Choose a compatible change",choices:[{choice_id:"keep",label:"Keep the field"},{choice_id:"remove",label:"Remove the field"}]}));

92: }
93: function query(sql:string):Record<string,unknown>|undefined {const db=openDb({path:daemon!.dbPath});try{return db.prepare(sql).get() as Record<string,unknown>|undefined;}finally{db.close();}}
94: async function adopt(){const result=await rpc("engineering.adopt",{credential,scope_id:scope,task_id:"task",run_id:"run",session_id:"session",goal:"Observe one reply turn",request_id:"adopt"});expect(result).toMatchObject({ok:true});return result.result!["mission"] as {mission_id:string;revision:number};}

96: async function answer(mission_id:string){const view=await observe(mission_id);const wait=(view["waits"] as Array<Record<string,unknown>>)[0]!;return {credential,mission_id,wait_id:wait["wait_id"],if_revision:(view["mission"] as {revision:number}).revision,decision_ref:wait["decision_ref"],action_id:"answer",answer:{kind:"choice",choice_id:"keep"},interaction_token:wait["interaction_token"]};}

===== FILE packages/cli/src/engineering-upgrade-guard.ts sha256=909b03a706135e0b70904063d82938e3c1f7b4e783f68e0cc2a1af92eef2c7df =====
1: import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
2: import { isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch } from "@osstack/core";
3: 
4: /** Pure read used before stopping a daemon and again under the stopped-home
5:  * database lock. Only proven completion or proven non-dispatch clears a hold. */
6: export function engineeringUpgradeHolds(db: Database.Database): number {
7:   if (!db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='attention_dispatch_phases'").get()) return 0;
8:   const rows = db.prepare(`SELECT p.message_id,p.phase,m.state
9:     FROM attention_dispatch_phases p LEFT JOIN session_messages m ON m.message_id=p.message_id`).all() as Array<{
10:     message_id: string; phase: string; state: string | null;
11:   }>;
12:   let holds = 0;
13:   for (const row of rows) {
14:     if (row.phase === "dispatch_committed" && row.state === "applied") continue;
15:     if (row.phase === "cancelled_before_dispatch") {
16:       try { if (isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(db, row.message_id)) continue; }
17:       catch { /* Missing or malformed proof dependencies remain held. */ }
18:     }
19:     holds++;
20:   }
21:   return holds;
22: }

115:     const { default: Database } = await import('better-sqlite3'); this.assertOperation();

117:     try {

120:       // Deliberately qualified, not CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION: a future migration
121:       // needs its own stopped-home compatibility review before this gate opens.

139:   private async doSnapshot(home: UpgradePlanHome, signal: AbortSignal, suffix = '') {

171:   /** Fresh explicit approval performs forward repair from observed facts, never a blind rerun.
172:    * A successful target is kept; absent services alone may be bootstrapped. Unknown observations stop. */

174:   private async doReconcileForward(plan: FrozenUpgradePlan, group: UpgradePlanGroup, journal: UpgradeJournal, expectedHistory: string, signal: AbortSignal) {

238:   /** Kernel locks may be released; the persistent operation fence survives every failure. */

241:   private async doComplete(): Promise<void> {

244:     const operation=join(this.config.root,'upgrade-operation.json'),permit=join(this.config.root,'restart-permit.json');

247:     if(existsSync(permit)){const bytes=readPrivate(permit);if(fenced){const value=JSON.parse(bytes.toString());if(value.plan_digest!==this.plan.digest||value.build_digest!==this.plan.plan.target.build_digest)throw Error('upgrade_restart_permit_binding');}await finishPrivatePublication(permit);}
248:     // Make absence of the blocking fence durable before removing startup permission.
249:     // A crash at either boundary is safe and the same authorized cleanup is resumable.
250:     await unlink(operation).catch(e=>{if(e.code!=='ENOENT')throw e;});await syncDirectory(this.config.root);
251:     await unlink(permit).catch(e=>{if(e.code!=='ENOENT')throw e;});await syncDirectory(this.config.root);

1: import { z } from 'zod';
2: import { DeploymentIdentitySchema, type DeploymentIdentity } from './home-inventory.js';
3: import { UpgradeDigest, UpgradeId, freezeUpgradePlan, upgradeCanonical, upgradeDigest, type FrozenUpgradePlan, type UpgradePlanGroup, type UpgradePlanHome } from './upgrade-plan.js';
4: import type { UpgradeJournal, UpgradeJournalWriter, UpgradePhase } from './upgrade-journal.js';
