6: const ref = z.string().min(1).max(512);
7: const AuthoritySchema = z.object({ plan_digest: UpgradeDigest, group_id: UpgradeId, installation_id: UpgradeId,
8:   consumer_home_ids: z.array(z.string()).min(1).max(32), expires_at_ms: z.number().int().nonnegative().safe(),
9:   completeness_evidence_ref: ref, qualification_ref: ref, authorization_ref: ref }).strict();
10: export type UpgradeAuthority = z.infer<typeof AuthoritySchema> & { assertCurrent: () => void };
11: const StoppedSchema = z.object({ home_id: z.string(), generation: z.string(), stopped: z.literal(true),
12:   graph_holds: z.literal(0), unknown_actions: z.literal(0), receipt_ref: ref }).strict();
13: export type UpgradeStopLease = { receipt: z.infer<typeof StoppedSchema>; assertHeld: () => void };
14: const ReceiptSchema = z.object({ home_id: z.string(), source_generation: z.string(), receipt_ref: ref }).strict();
15: const InstallSchema = z.object({ plan_digest: UpgradeDigest, installation_id: UpgradeId, version: z.string(), build_digest: UpgradeDigest, receipt_ref: ref }).strict();
16: 
17: /** Trusted host boundary. ManagedNativeUpgradePort implements the opt-in pinned launchd backend.
18:  * Implementations must prove the complete controlled consumer set independently of inventory. */
19: export interface NativeUpgradePort {
20:   resolveAuthority(plan: FrozenUpgradePlan, group: UpgradePlanGroup, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<UpgradeAuthority | null>;
21:   observe(home: UpgradePlanHome, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<{ identity: DeploymentIdentity; healthy: boolean; receipt_ref: string }>;
22:   drain(home: UpgradePlanHome, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<UpgradeStopLease>;
23:   snapshot(home: UpgradePlanHome, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<z.infer<typeof ReceiptSchema>>;
24:   install(plan: FrozenUpgradePlan, group: UpgradePlanGroup, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<z.infer<typeof InstallSchema>>;
25:   restart(home: UpgradePlanHome, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<z.infer<typeof ReceiptSchema>>;
26: }
27: export interface UpgradeExecutionResult { state: UpgradePhase; plan_digest: string; group_id: string; reason: string | null }
28: function synchronous(call: () => void): void {
29:   const value: unknown = call(); if (value !== undefined) { void Promise.resolve(value).catch(() => {}); throw Error('upgrade_async_authority'); }
30: }
31: 
32: /** Runs only with an explicit trusted port. A persisted plan never grants permission.
33:  * Every side effect has a durable started record; incomplete prior attempts are never replayed. */
34: export async function coordinateUpgrade(input: {
35:   journal: UpgradeJournal; plan: FrozenUpgradePlan; groupId: string; port?: NativeUpgradePort;
36:   nowMs?: () => number; monotonicMs?: () => number; signal?: AbortSignal;
37: }): Promise<UpgradeExecutionResult> {
38:   const plan = freezeUpgradePlan(input.plan.plan); if (plan.digest !== input.plan.digest) throw Error('upgrade_plan_digest_mismatch');
39:   const group = plan.plan.groups.find(g => g.group_id === input.groupId); if (!group) throw Error('upgrade_group_missing');
40:   const result = (state: UpgradePhase, reason: string | null = null): UpgradeExecutionResult => ({ state, plan_digest: plan.digest, group_id: group.group_id, reason });
41:   await input.journal.create(plan); const prior = await input.journal.status(plan.digest, group.group_id);
42:   if (prior.completed) return result('verified');
43:   if (prior.phase === 'recovery_required' || prior.records.some(r => r.phase !== 'qualification_pending')) return result('recovery_required', 'prior_attempt_requires_reconciliation');
44:   if (!input.port) return result('qualification_pending', 'native_updater_not_qualified');
45:   for (const previous of plan.plan.groups.slice(0, plan.plan.groups.indexOf(group))) {
46:     if (!(await input.journal.status(plan.digest, previous.group_id)).completed) return result('qualification_pending', 'preceding_group_not_verified');
47:   }
48:   const unqualifiedIdentity = group.homes.some(home => !home.identity || home.identity.home_id !== home.home_id
49:     || home.identity.deployment_kind !== 'npm' || home.identity.build_digest === null || home.identity.build_evidence !== 'host_manifest'
50:     || home.identity.database_schema > plan.plan.target.database_schema
51:     || home.identity.database_schema < plan.plan.target.readable_schema_min || home.identity.database_schema > plan.plan.target.readable_schema_max
52:     || home.observed_at_ms > plan.plan.created_at_ms || plan.plan.created_at_ms - home.observed_at_ms > 30000);
53:   if (plan.plan.budget.max_steps < 5 * group.homes.length + 2 || group.deployment_kind !== 'npm' || unqualifiedIdentity || group.reasons.some(reason => !['shared_consumers_not_attested', 'native_updater_not_qualified'].includes(reason)))
54:     return result('qualification_pending', 'plan_has_unqualified_consumers');
55:   const port = input.port; const now = input.nowMs ?? Date.now; const mono = input.monotonicMs ?? (() => performance.now());
56:   const startedAt = mono(); let steps = 0; let authority: UpgradeAuthority | undefined; let writer: UpgradeJournalWriter | undefined;
57:   let phase: UpgradePhase = 'preflight'; let installStarted = false; let sideEffectStarted = false; let closed = false;
58:   const stops: UpgradeStopLease[] = [];
59:   const clockCheck = () => {
60:     if (closed || input.signal?.aborted) throw Error('upgrade_cancelled');
61:     if (now() < plan.plan.created_at_ms || now() >= plan.plan.expires_at_ms) throw Error('upgrade_plan_expired');
62:     if (mono() - startedAt >= plan.plan.budget.max_total_ms) throw Error('upgrade_budget_exhausted');
63:     if (authority) {
64:       if (now() >= authority.expires_at_ms) throw Error('upgrade_authority_expired'); synchronous(authority.assertCurrent);
65:     }
66:   };
67:   const append = async (status: 'started' | 'confirmed' | 'failed', home: UpgradePlanHome | null, evidence?: unknown, evidenceRef?: string, reason?: string) => {
68:     if (!writer) return;
69:     await writer.append({ phase, status, at_ms: now(), home_id: home?.home_id ?? null, evidence_digest: evidence === undefined ? null : upgradeDigest(evidence),
70:       evidence_ref: evidenceRef ?? null, reason: reason ?? null });
71:   };
72:   const call = async <T>(operation: (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<T>, mutating = false): Promise<T> => {
73:     clockCheck(); if (steps >= plan.plan.budget.max_steps) throw Error('upgrade_budget_exhausted'); steps += 1;
74:     const controller = new AbortController(); let rejectAbort!: (error: Error) => void;
75:     const cancelled = new Promise<never>((_, reject) => { rejectAbort = reject; });
76:     const abort = () => { controller.abort(); rejectAbort(Error('upgrade_cancelled')); };
77:     input.signal?.addEventListener('abort', abort, { once: true }); if (input.signal?.aborted) abort();
78:     const remaining = plan.plan.budget.max_total_ms - (mono() - startedAt);
79:     let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
80:     try {
81:       const deadline = new Promise<never>((_, reject) => { timer = setTimeout(() => { controller.abort(); reject(Error('upgrade_operation_unknown')); }, Math.max(1, Math.min(remaining, plan.plan.budget.max_step_ms))); });
82:       // After timeout, the late operation cannot append or advance phases. Its physical effects remain unknown.
83:       const value = await Promise.race([Promise.resolve().then(() => { clockCheck(); if (mutating) synchronous(writer!.assertCurrent); return operation(controller.signal); }), deadline, cancelled]);
84:       clockCheck(); return value;
85:     } finally { if (timer) clearTimeout(timer); input.signal?.removeEventListener('abort', abort); }
86:   };
87:   try {
88:     const resolved = await call(signal => port.resolveAuthority(plan, group, signal));
89:     if (!resolved) return result('qualification_pending', 'shared_consumers_not_attested');
90:     const { assertCurrent, ...raw } = resolved; const parsed = AuthoritySchema.parse(raw);
91:     if (typeof assertCurrent !== 'function' || parsed.plan_digest !== plan.digest || parsed.group_id !== group.group_id || parsed.installation_id !== group.installation_id
92:       || upgradeCanonical([...parsed.consumer_home_ids].sort()) !== upgradeCanonical(group.homes.map(h => h.home_id).sort())) throw Error('upgrade_authority_binding');
93:     authority = { ...parsed, assertCurrent: assertCurrent.bind(resolved) }; clockCheck();
94:     writer = await input.journal.claim(plan.digest, group.group_id); clockCheck(); await append('started', null, parsed, parsed.authorization_ref);
95:     for (const home of group.homes) {
96:       const observation = await call(signal => port.observe(home, signal));
97:       const identity = DeploymentIdentitySchema.parse(observation.identity);
98:       if (observation.healthy !== true || upgradeCanonical(identity) !== upgradeCanonical(home.identity)) throw Error('upgrade_identity_changed');
99:       ref.parse(observation.receipt_ref); await append('confirmed', home, identity, observation.receipt_ref); clockCheck();
100:     }
101:     phase = 'draining';
102:     for (const home of group.homes) {
103:       await append('started', home); clockCheck(); sideEffectStarted = true;
104:       const lease = await call(signal => port.drain(home, signal), true); const receipt = StoppedSchema.parse(lease.receipt);
105:       if (receipt.home_id !== home.home_id || receipt.generation !== home.identity?.generation || typeof lease.assertHeld !== 'function') throw Error('upgrade_stop_binding');
106:       const frozenLease = { receipt, assertHeld: lease.assertHeld.bind(lease) }; synchronous(frozenLease.assertHeld); stops.push(frozenLease);
107:       await append('confirmed', home, receipt, receipt.receipt_ref); clockCheck();
108:     }
109:     const quiescent = () => { clockCheck(); for (const stop of stops) synchronous(stop.assertHeld); if (stops.length !== group.homes.length) throw Error('upgrade_all_consumers_required'); };
110:     quiescent(); phase = 'quiescent'; await append('confirmed', null, stops.map(s => s.receipt)); quiescent(); phase = 'snapshotting';
111:     for (const home of group.homes) {
112:       await append('started', home); quiescent(); const receipt = ReceiptSchema.parse(await call(signal => { quiescent(); return port.snapshot(home, signal); }, true));
113:       quiescent(); if (receipt.home_id !== home.home_id || receipt.source_generation !== home.identity?.generation) throw Error('upgrade_snapshot_binding');
114:       await append('confirmed', home, receipt, receipt.receipt_ref); quiescent();
115:     }
116:     phase = 'installing'; await append('started', null); quiescent(); installStarted = true;
117:     const installed = InstallSchema.parse(await call(signal => { quiescent(); return port.install(plan, group, signal); }, true)); quiescent();
118:     if (installed.plan_digest !== plan.digest || installed.installation_id !== group.installation_id || installed.version !== plan.plan.target.version || installed.build_digest !== plan.plan.target.build_digest) throw Error('upgrade_install_mismatch');
119:     phase = 'installed'; await append('confirmed', null, installed, installed.receipt_ref); clockCheck(); phase = 'restarting';
120:     for (const home of group.homes) {
121:       await append('started', home); clockCheck(); synchronous(stops[group.homes.indexOf(home)]!.assertHeld);
122:       const receipt = ReceiptSchema.parse(await call(signal => { synchronous(stops[group.homes.indexOf(home)]!.assertHeld); return port.restart(home, signal); }, true));
123:       if (receipt.home_id !== home.home_id || receipt.source_generation !== home.identity?.generation) throw Error('upgrade_restart_binding');
124:       await append('confirmed', home, receipt, receipt.receipt_ref); clockCheck();
125:     }
126:     phase = 'verifying';
127:     for (const home of group.homes) {
128:       await append('started', home); clockCheck(); const observed = await call(signal => port.observe(home, signal)); const identity = DeploymentIdentitySchema.parse(observed.identity);
129:       if (observed.healthy !== true || identity.home_id !== home.home_id || identity.generation === home.identity?.generation || identity.deployment_kind !== 'npm'
130:         || identity.version !== plan.plan.target.version || identity.build_digest !== plan.plan.target.build_digest || identity.build_evidence !== 'host_manifest'
131:         || identity.protocol_version !== plan.plan.target.protocol_version || identity.database_schema !== plan.plan.target.database_schema) throw Error('upgrade_version_mismatch');
132:       ref.parse(observed.receipt_ref); await append('confirmed', home, identity, observed.receipt_ref); clockCheck();
133:     }
134:     phase = 'verified'; clockCheck(); await append('confirmed', null);
135:     try { await writer.releaseVerified(); } catch { closed = true; return result('verified', 'claim_release_requires_reconciliation'); }
136:     closed = true; return result('verified');
137:   } catch (error) {
138:     if (error instanceof Error && error.message === 'upgrade_already_verified') return result('verified');
139:     const message = error instanceof Error && /^upgrade_[a-z_]+$/.test(error.message) ? error.message : 'upgrade_port_failure';
140:     if (!writer && message !== 'upgrade_recovery_required') {
141:       const status = await input.journal.status(plan.digest, group.group_id).catch(() => null);
142:       if (status?.completed) return result('verified', status.recovery.claim_cleanup_pending ? 'claim_release_requires_reconciliation' : null);
143:       return result(!status || status.phase === 'recovery_required' ? 'recovery_required' : 'qualification_pending', message);
144:     }
145:     const state: UpgradePhase = message === 'upgrade_recovery_required' || sideEffectStarted && !installStarted ? 'recovery_required'
146:       : !installStarted ? 'aborted_before_install' : message === 'upgrade_version_mismatch' ? 'version_mismatch' : 'recovery_required';
147:     phase = state; closed = true;
148:     // A failed journal write leaves its previous started record. Never undo an external fact or steal the claim.
149:     try { await append('failed', null, undefined, undefined, message); } catch { /* Caller must inspect the retained claim and previous record. */ }
150:     return result(state, message);
151:   }
152: }

===== FILE docs/guides/engineering-session-adoption.en.md sha256=9e0e29abc0c59e2ce5dd5c31812996e4a684fc00da226ed3cc7bdc98ee31bea5 =====
1: # Adopt a Session and handle a waiting decision
2: 
3: [中文](engineering-session-adoption.md)
4: 
5: `osstack engineering` provides one entry to adopt an existing Session, inspect waits, send an explicit answer, and observe its reply turn. This local pilot requires explicit enablement. It does not start an initial Agent, select Loop or Graph, or provide a general chat interface.
6: 
7: The completion level is `observed_turn`. OSStack confirms that the reply turn caused by this Mission's answer completed and was stored. This result does not prove code delivery, passing checks, or successful deployment.
8: 
9: An exact decision can currently be adopted by only one Mission, once. This remains true if observation ends or is cancelled before any answer. A later adoption returns `authority_already_adopted`. A cancelled answer cannot be replaced with a new key. Continue through the original Session endpoint, or handle a new decision with a new source-issued identity. Do not fabricate a decision ID to bypass this limit.
10: 
11: ## 1. Enable the pilot
12: 
13: Use an exact, admitted Task, Run, and Session. The Session must be `awaiting_decision` and have a nonempty native session ID. The pilot accepts only the default tenant and ordinary product choices or questions that allow text input. Risk and task authorization still use their existing approval endpoints.
14: 
15: The host sets both `OSSTACK_ATTENTION=1` and `OSSTACK_ENGINEERING=1`. Set an explicit, isolated `OSSTACK_HOME`. Do not open `~/.osstack` or a reserved production home from a development checkout. This command does not start a daemon.
16: 
17: Use the existing attention service to establish the exact repository scope:
18: 
19: ```sh
20: osstack attention watch --task TASK_ID --repo /absolute/repository --default-tenant
21: ```
22: 
23: Save the returned `scope_id`. Check the Session's Task, Run, and repository binding. The host operator prepares the authority file, then restarts that home's daemon with the existing operations procedure. The pilot has no command that automatically grants host authority.
24: 
25: ### Host identity configuration
26: 
27: `OSSTACK_ENGINEERING_AUTH_FILE` is an absolute path. Its file must belong to the current host user, have mode `0600`, and be a regular file, not a symbolic link. Keep it in a trusted private home and outside every adopted repository. Do not commit it.
28: 
29: The file has this structure. Replace all placeholders. Digests and scopes are 64 lowercase hexadecimal characters.
30: 
31: ```json
32: {
33:   "version": 1,
34:   "principals": [
35:     {
36:       "credential_sha256": "<SHA256 of owner credential>",
37:       "principal_id": "local-owner",
38:       "client_session_id": "owner-cli",
39:       "read_scopes": ["<scope_id>"],
40:       "write_scopes": ["<scope_id>"],
41:       "stop_scopes": ["<scope_id>"],
42:       "operator_scopes": []
43:     },
44:     {
45:       "credential_sha256": "<SHA256 of separate operator credential>",
46:       "principal_id": "local-stop-operator",
47:       "client_session_id": "operator-cli",
48:       "read_scopes": ["<scope_id>"],
49:       "write_scopes": [],
50:       "stop_scopes": ["<scope_id>"],
51:       "operator_scopes": ["<scope_id>"]
52:     }
53:   ]
54: }
55: ```
56: 
57: Use separate, cryptographically random credentials. Provision each raw credential through a trusted channel outside the repository into the client environment variable `OSSTACK_ENGINEERING_CREDENTIAL`. The CLI reads that variable only. It does not select a principal from the authority file. Do not place credentials in command arguments, request JSON, logs, reports, or the repository. The authority file stores SHA-256 digests only. The host reloads grants on each request and before first dispatch; revocation does not depend on a stale cache.
58: 
59: Rotating `credential_sha256` while retaining the same principal/client and grants does not revoke that principal's accepted answer awaiting first dispatch. To block dispatch, remove its write grant or its principal/client authority row. The host rechecks principal authority before dispatch; a changed credential hash is not grant revocation. Keep a separately authenticated operator with read + stop authority for recovery.
60: 
61: This is a trusted-process profile. A token file does not isolate a hostile Worker with the same UID. Existing local socket endpoints, including `session.send`, `attention.answer`, `attention.abandon-receipt`, and `task.cancel`, can still operate on the same Session under their original authority rules. Engineering credentials do not replace those rules. Every local caller must be trusted. This pilot does not establish production isolation qualification.
62: 
63: ## 2. Observe one reply
64: 
65: Run these commands with the same private home and authenticated client. Save every `--request` or `--action` idempotency key. After a timeout, retry the unchanged request with the same key. Do not change the key to guess the outcome.
66: 
67: `action_id` uses the home's global attention namespace, shared by all principals and legacy commands. Generate and save a globally unique UUID for each new answer, or use its actual Mission UUID with a suffix such as `actualMissionUUID:answer-001`. Do not reuse plain `answer-001` across Missions. `request_id` is unique within the principal/client pair.
68: 
69: ```sh
70: osstack engineering adopt \
71:   --scope SCOPE_ID --task TASK_ID --run RUN_ID --session SESSION_ID \
72:   --goal "Observe the reply to this compatibility decision" --request adopt-001 --json
73: 
74: osstack engineering observe --mission MISSION_ID --json > "$OSSTACK_HOME/mission-view.json"
75: ```
76: 
77: Read the question, explicit choice IDs, Mission revision, Wait ID, and state in this file. The file contains a short-lived interaction token. Protect the file and do not commit it. Send an explicit choice. Generate the following UUID once. Preserve `ANSWER_ID` for retries and queries:
78: 
79: ```sh
80: ANSWER_ID="$(node -p 'require("node:crypto").randomUUID()')"
81: osstack engineering decide --observation "$OSSTACK_HOME/mission-view.json" \
82:   --wait WAIT_ID --action "$ANSWER_ID" --choice CHOICE_ID --json
83: 
84: osstack engineering observe --mission MISSION_ID --json
85: ```
86: 
87: For a question that allows original text, use `--text "original answer"` instead of `--choice`. Do not supply both. The system does not map “continue” to an assumed choice. Text does not authorize a risk action.
88: 
89: `--observation` uses the exact Mission revision, decision reference, and token in the saved file. The CLI does not silently refresh them before submission. The host checks current source identity, grants, and expiry again.
90: 
91: Interpret each state by its evidence:
92: 
93: | State or fact | Meaning |
94: |---|---|
95: | Answer recorded / reserved | The answer reservation is durable. Worker execution is not yet proven. |
96: | dispatch_committed | This dispatch is committed. An unknown result cannot cause an automatic resend. |
97: | applied | The full reply turn completed and was stored. This is more than transport acceptance. |
98: | delivery_unknown / needs_reconciliation | Evidence is incomplete. The original receipt and responsibility remain. |
99: | Mission completed + ObservationProof | The exact reply turn is confirmed, with no new decision, unresolved wait, unknown effect, or cancellation intent. |
100: 
101: If the reply asks a new question, observe again and handle the new Wait. An older terminal Run, plain `done` text, or an idle Session cannot independently complete this Mission.
102: 
103: ## 3. Defer, cancel, and end observation
104: 
105: Deferral only changes the recheck schedule. It does not answer or grant permission.
106: 
107: ```sh
108: osstack engineering defer --observation "$OSSTACK_HOME/mission-view.json" \
109:   --wait WAIT_ID --request defer-001 \
110:   --revisit-at 2026-10-26T12:00:00Z --reason "Waiting for product input"
111: 
112: osstack engineering cancel --mission MISSION_ID --revision REVISION \
113:   --request cancel-001 --reason "Stop this work"
114: 
115: osstack engineering end-observation --mission MISSION_ID --revision REVISION \
116:   --request end-001 --reason "Stop observing this reply"
117: ```
118: 
119: `cancel` stores a stop intent and requests an abort through an independent stop path. An unknown answer does not block it. Neither `stop_requested` nor `stop_unknown` proves termination. An action already in flight can still complete.
120: 
121: `end-observation` ends this Mission's observation and prevents its later first dispatch. It does not stop the Worker, claim success, or erase committed/unknown receipts. The original attention reconciliation responsibility and Session send guard remain active. If the original answer is still waiting for repository writer admission, the host releases that queued work only after durable proof confirms `cancelled_before_dispatch`. This releases work that never started; it does not abort a committed Worker.
122: 
123: When cancellation proves that dispatch never occurred, replay of the original answer with the same key and payload returns `cancelled_before_dispatch`. A changed payload returns `action_conflict`. A new action_id submitted after the Mission ends returns `mission_terminal`. The legacy `session.send` endpoint returns the error code `cancelled_original_answer` for replay of that cancelled message. None of these outcomes permits reopening or replacing the original answer.
124: 
125: | Operation | Current authority required |
126: |---|---|
127: | adopt | Scope read + write; records an immutable owner |
128: | decide / defer / dispose_wait | That owner with current read + write |
129: | cancel | Current scope read + independent stop, and either the owner or an explicit scope-stop operator |
130: | end-observation | Owner with read authority, or operator with read + stop |
131: 
132: Other writers cannot replace the owner. Write authority does not imply stop authority. An owner can still cancel after write revocation if read and independent stop remain valid. Read revocation prevents Mission access; a separately authenticated operator provides recovery. If the whole credential is revoked, a separately authenticated scope-stop operator provides recovery.
133: 
134: ## 4. Reconcile
135: 
136: Query the original action. This query does not resend it:
137: 
138: ```sh
139: osstack engineering reconcile --kind query_action \
140:   --mission MISSION_ID --action "$ANSWER_ID" --json
141: ```
142: 
143: If a person handled the question in the original Session, and the old Wait has no answer action or unknown effect in flight, explicitly remove that observation obligation. First read a fresh snapshot:
144: 
145: ```sh
146: osstack engineering observe --mission MISSION_ID --json > "$OSSTACK_HOME/mission-view.json"
147: osstack engineering reconcile --kind dispose_wait \
148:   --observation "$OSSTACK_HOME/mission-view.json" --wait WAIT_ID --request dispose-001 \
149:   --disposition human_answered_out_of_band --reason "Handled in the original Session"
150: ```
151: 
152: The other disposition is `no_longer_relevant`. Disposal binds the exact old Wait and current observation digest. It is an authorized change to the observation goal. It does not create applied evidence, termination evidence, or execution authority. A Wait with an answer action must use receipt query or cancellation; a human statement cannot clear unknown effects.
153: 
154: ## 5. Interface and limits
155: 
156: Every command accepts `--input FILE` instead of its business flags. `--json` can be used with it. The request must be strict JSON in a regular, non-symlink file of at most 1 MiB. Unknown fields, duplicate flags, and `actor` or authority claims are rejected. Zod definitions and generated JSON Schemas are in `packages/protocol/src/engineering.ts` and `packages/protocol/schemas/engineering-*.schema.json`.
157: 
158: For text that starts with `--`, such as `--help`, use `--input FILE` with the complete request JSON. Set its `answer` to `{"kind":"text","text":"--help"}`. This fragment is not a complete request. `--text -h` works; `--text --help` is rejected as a missing value.
159: 
160: For `observe`, `--cursor` is an event sequence and `--limit` is 1–100. Check cursor, has_more, and unresolved Waits. Incremental events do not replace the current wait view. Restrict permissions on saved JSON that contains interaction tokens.
161: 
162: | Error | Next action |
163: |---|---|
164: | permission_denied | Ask the host operator to check current identity and exact scope grants. Do not add an actor claim to JSON. |
165: | stale_decision / source_changed | Observe again. Query the old action before creating a new answer, so an accepted effect is not duplicated. |
166: | action_conflict / action_id_conflict | Preserve the original key and payload. Use a separate request for a distinct intent. |
167: | delivery_unknown | Query the original action. Do not resend with a new key. |
168: | signed_approval_required | Use the formal risk/task approval endpoint. |
169: | executor_not_qualified | Check default tenancy, Session state, native ID, and the supported Adapter profile. |
170: | not_dispatched_use_cancel | Cancel a proven uncommitted reservation. Do not classify it as an unknown sent message. |
171: | cancelled_original_answer | The original message was cancelled before dispatch. Query its receipt; do not resend with a new key. |
172: | authority_already_adopted | This exact decision was already adopted, including observation ended/cancelled without an answer. Use the original Mission/Session endpoint. |
173: | budget_exhausted | A persistent host or Mission limit was reached. Inspect the ledger; do not reset the database. This home currently permits 1000 cumulative Missions, including terminal records. |
174: | engineering_busy | Dispatch slots are full. Retry the unchanged request later. Read and stop remain available. |
175: | mission_terminal | The Mission ended. Read its outcome; do not turn a retry into a new goal or answer. |
176: | interaction_expired | Query the original action first. If it was never accepted, observe again for a current token and submit an explicit new request. |
177: | interaction_mismatch | Check the principal/client and original observation file. Do not combine tokens, Waits, or decision references manually. |
178: | durability_required | The actual daemon write connection is not verified as synchronous=FULL. Correct host configuration; do not bypass the durability check. |
179: | stop_attempt_budget_exhausted | This Mission has accepted eight stop attempts. The first stop intent remains. Query its receipt and ask the host operator to resolve it; a new key cannot bypass the limit. |
180: 
181: The host permits at most 16 concurrent in-memory dispatch attempts and scans at most 16 reserved actions and 16 active Missions per watchdog page. A reply has a 5-minute host deadline. Expiry requests an abort; it does not prove process termination. Each deferral produces at most one reminder event, visible through `observe`; this pilot sends no external notifications.
182: 
183: The pilot stores at most 1,000 Mission records per home and 64 Waits per Mission. Each Mission permits 256 general control requests. Cancellation and ending observation have separate limits: cancellation permits at most eight accepted stop attempts per Mission, including the first. Each attempt keeps the first stop intent and targets only that Mission's unresolved original committed messages. An identical replay does not consume another attempt. Ending observation is a single terminal operation. Interaction tokens expire after five minutes; a Mission retains at most 32 active tokens. Neither `budget_exhausted` nor `stop_attempt_budget_exhausted` authorizes deletion of unresolved evidence. Query the receipt and ask the host operator to inspect the limit.
184: 
185: `completed` and `failed` are absorbing states. A new cancel or end-observation request returns `mission_terminal`; it cannot change the outcome or abort a later manual instruction in the same Session. An accepted identical request remains available as a read-only replay. After observation ends, the independent stop path can still request abort for this Mission's unresolved committed messages within the stop-attempt limit.
186: 
187: `cost_evidence` reports `status: unknown` and `hard_cap_enforced: false`. The adoption pilot does not meter Provider usage or treat missing usage as zero. The 5-minute host deadline is not a token or cost limit. A real coding reply can exceed it. An abort can leave an unknown receipt and partial workspace edits. This profile is not suitable for unbounded long reply turns.
188: 
189: ### Disable and recover
190: 
191: Disabling `OSSTACK_ENGINEERING` does not erase dispatch phases or release the Session guard. An existing `reserved` action still blocks new manual instructions, and legacy `abandon-receipt` requires cancellation first. The disabled engineering endpoint cannot cancel it. Re-enable `OSSTACK_ENGINEERING=1` and `OSSTACK_ATTENTION=1` for the same home, then use a valid credential to query and resolve the original action before disabling again. Do not rewrite or delete database receipts to unlock it. A committed/unknown action still requires its original reconciliation and must not be resent after re-enablement.
192: 
193: ### Upgrade, rollback, and service recovery
194: 
195: Schema 47 is a database migration boundary. After migration, a binary that supports only the old schema cannot open this home. Reinstalling an older npm version or replacing only the binary is not a valid rollback. This pilot has no supported database downgrade or automatic rollback path. A backup alone does not authorize rollback: retain post-migration actions and evidence of external effects.
196: 
197: Upgrade checks `attention_dispatch_holds`. A hold clears only when the original committed message is `applied`, or complete proof establishes `cancelled_before_dispatch`. A committed message without applied evidence continues to block upgrade. A failed or unknown receipt, `abandoned_unknown`, ending observation, or disabling the feature cannot clear it. For a failed or unknown action without original applied evidence, the hold can persist indefinitely. This pilot has no supported automatic release procedure. Preserve the home, ledger, and available effect evidence for a separately designed and reviewed offline recovery procedure. Do not edit phases, messages, receipts, or the schema version, and do not delete the database to force an upgrade.
198: 
199: Even when `daemon.drain` returns `drained: false`, the daemon remains draining. It refuses new tasks and Session writes. An aborted upgrade does not automatically restore admission. First confirm that no install, restart, or other maintenance is still running. Then inspect the hold. An original reserved answer with proof that dispatch never occurred can be explicitly cancelled while draining. An unknown committed action cannot be released this way.
200: 
201: If you abandon this upgrade and keep running the current version, a trusted host operator must call the `daemon.resume` RPC. Then check that `osstack health` reports `result.draining: false`. This version has no `osstack daemon resume` or generic `osstack rpc` command. The following Node command uses the existing local socket RPC and does not open the database. Replace `OSSTACK_SOCKET` with the exact absolute socket path for this home from its daemon startup log. Keep `OSSTACK_HOME` set to that home:
202: 
203: ```sh
204: export OSSTACK_SOCKET=/absolute/path/from-daemon-startup.sock
205: node --input-type=module <<'NODE'
206: import { connect } from 'node:net';
207: import { isAbsolute } from 'node:path';
208: const path = process.env.OSSTACK_SOCKET;
209: if (!path || !isAbsolute(path)) throw Error('Set OSSTACK_SOCKET to the exact absolute daemon socket path');
210: const socket = connect(path);
211: let bytes = Buffer.alloc(0), done = false;
212: const fail = message => { if (done) return; done = true; clearTimeout(timer); socket.destroy(); console.error(message); process.exitCode = 1; };
213: const timer = setTimeout(() => fail('daemon.resume timed out'), 5000);
214: socket.on('error', error => fail(error.message));
215: socket.on('end', () => { if (!done) fail('Incomplete RPC response'); });
216: socket.on('connect', () => {
217:   const body = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ id: 1, method: 'daemon.resume' }));
218:   const header = Buffer.alloc(4); header.writeUInt32BE(body.length);
219:   socket.write(Buffer.concat([header, body]));
220: });
221: socket.on('data', chunk => {
222:   bytes = Buffer.concat([bytes, chunk]);
223:   if (bytes.length < 4) return;
224:   const size = bytes.readUInt32BE(0);
225:   if (size > 1048576) return fail('RPC response exceeds 1 MiB');
226:   if (bytes.length < size + 4) return;
227:   try {
228:     const response = JSON.parse(bytes.subarray(4, size + 4).toString('utf8'));
229:     if (response.id !== 1 || response.ok !== true || response.result?.draining !== false) throw Error('Resume was not confirmed');
230:     done = true; clearTimeout(timer); socket.end(); console.log(JSON.stringify(response));
231:   } catch (error) { fail(error.message); }
232: });
233: NODE
234: osstack health
235: ```
236: 
237: Resuming admission lets eligible queued work run again. First cancel any reserved answer that must not execute. `daemon.resume` does not clear an upgrade hold, prove that unknown work stopped, or roll back the database. A bad health condition, such as low disk space, can raise draining again. This RPC uses the existing trusted local host interface; it does not use engineering credential authorization.
238: 
239: Independent checks still required: live Provider replies, physical power loss and filesystem durability, process-domain termination across restarts, hostile same-UID Worker isolation, nondefault tenants, and remote hosts. Unit tests, the full test suite, and model audits do not replace those qualifications.
240: 
241: See the [revised runtime contract](../design/2026-10-25-engineering-agent/runtime-contract-v2.md) and [pilot scenarios](../design/2026-10-25-engineering-agent/pilot-scenarios.json).

===== FILE docs/guides/engineering-session-adoption.md sha256=b9af7d34770ea06167de198364bb715aba631de962f32acf6bdd362011fb679f =====
1: # 采纳 Session，处理等待决策
2: 
3: [English](engineering-session-adoption.en.md)
4: 
5: `osstack engineering` 用同一个入口采纳已有 Session、显示等待、提交明确回答并观察回复回合。它是显式启用的本地试点。它不启动初始 Agent，不选择 Loop 或 Graph，也不提供通用聊天界面。
6: 
7: 完成级别固定为 `observed_turn`：OSStack 确认本 Mission 的回答所触发的回复回合已完成并落库。这个结果不是代码交付、测试通过或部署成功的证明。
8: 
9: 当前每个精确决定只能被一个 Mission 采纳一次。即使尚未回答就取消或结束观察，这个决定也不能再采纳；系统返回 `authority_already_adopted`。已有回答取消后也不能换键重答。继续用原 Session 的正式入口处理，或由来源产生具有新身份的新决定。不要伪造 decision ID 来绕过这个限制。
10: 
11: ## 1. 启用条件
12: 
13: 使用已经准入、处于 `awaiting_decision` 的精确 Task、Run 和 Session。Session 必须具有非空原生会话 ID。试点仅接受默认租户、普通产品选择或允许文本输入的问题。风险批准、任务授权仍使用原来的正式批准入口。
14: 
15: 宿主同时设置 `OSSTACK_ATTENTION=1` 和 `OSSTACK_ENGINEERING=1`。使用独立、明确的 `OSSTACK_HOME`。不要用开发 checkout 打开 `~/.osstack` 或保留的生产 home。CLI 不自动启动 daemon。
16: 
17: 先通过已有 attention 服务建立精确仓库范围：
18: 
19: ```sh
20: osstack attention watch --task TASK_ID --repo /absolute/repository --default-tenant
21: ```
22: 
23: 保存返回的 `scope_id`，并核对 Session 的 Task、Run 和仓库绑定。由宿主管理者准备权限文件，然后按现有运维流程重启该 home 的 daemon。试点没有自动生成宿主权限的命令。
24: 
25: ### 宿主身份配置
26: 
27: `OSSTACK_ENGINEERING_AUTH_FILE` 是绝对路径。文件必须由当前宿主用户拥有，权限为 `0600`，是普通文件且不是符号链接。将它放在受信私有 home 内，且位于所有被采纳仓库之外。不要提交它。
28: 
29: 文件结构如下。替换全部占位值。摘要和 scope 都是 64 位小写十六进制字符串。
30: 
31: ```json
32: {
33:   "version": 1,
34:   "principals": [
35:     {
36:       "credential_sha256": "<SHA256 of owner credential>",
37:       "principal_id": "local-owner",
38:       "client_session_id": "owner-cli",
39:       "read_scopes": ["<scope_id>"],
40:       "write_scopes": ["<scope_id>"],
41:       "stop_scopes": ["<scope_id>"],
42:       "operator_scopes": []
43:     },
44:     {
45:       "credential_sha256": "<SHA256 of separate operator credential>",
46:       "principal_id": "local-stop-operator",
47:       "client_session_id": "operator-cli",
48:       "read_scopes": ["<scope_id>"],
49:       "write_scopes": [],
50:       "stop_scopes": ["<scope_id>"],
51:       "operator_scopes": ["<scope_id>"]
52:     }
53:   ]
54: }
55: ```
56: 
57: 使用密码学随机的独立凭据。通过受信、仓库外的渠道将原始凭据传给客户端环境变量 `OSSTACK_ENGINEERING_CREDENTIAL`。CLI 只读这个环境变量；它不从权限文件挑选账号。不要把凭据放到命令参数、请求 JSON、日志、报告或仓库里。宿主文件只存 SHA-256 摘要。宿主在每次请求及首次派发前重新读取权限，撤销不会依赖旧缓存。
58: 
59: 仅轮换 `credential_sha256`，同时保留相同 principal/client 及权限，不会撤销这个主体已经接受、仍待首次派发的回答。要阻止派发，撤销对应 write grant 或删除该 principal/client 授权行。宿主在派发前重读主体授权，不把凭据哈希变化等同于撤权。保留独立的 read + stop operator 恢复通道。
60: 
61: 这是受信进程 profile。权限文件不能隔离同一 UID 下的恶意 Worker。旧的本地 socket 接口（例如 `session.send`、`attention.answer`、`attention.abandon-receipt`、`task.cancel`）仍可按其原有权限规则操作同一 Session；engineering 凭据不覆盖这些入口的授权。所有本地调用者必须可信。试点未因此获得生产隔离资格。
62: 
63: ## 2. 完成一次回复观察
64: 
65: 以下命令在同一个私有 home、同一个客户端身份下执行。每个 `--request` 或 `--action` 都是调用者保存的幂等键。发生超时后，重试原请求与原键；不要换键猜测结果。
66: 
67: `action_id` 使用同一 home 的 attention 全局命名空间，多个主体和旧命令共享它。每次新回答生成并保存一个全局唯一 UUID，或使用真实 Mission UUID 加后缀（如 `真实MissionUUID:answer-001`）。不能让不同 Mission 都使用裸 `answer-001`。`request_id` 则在 principal/client 范围内唯一。
68: 
69: ```sh
70: osstack engineering adopt \
71:   --scope SCOPE_ID --task TASK_ID --run RUN_ID --session SESSION_ID \
72:   --goal "确认本次兼容性选择的回复回合" --request adopt-001 --json
73: 
74: osstack engineering observe --mission MISSION_ID --json > "$OSSTACK_HOME/mission-view.json"
75: ```
76: 
77: 读取这个观察文件中的问题、明确选项 ID、Mission revision、Wait ID 和状态。文件保存了短期交互 token。保护这个文件，不要提交它。随后回答明确选项。下列 UUID 只生成一次，重试和查询必须复用保存的 `ANSWER_ID`：
78: 
79: ```sh
80: ANSWER_ID="$(node -p 'require("node:crypto").randomUUID()')"
81: osstack engineering decide --observation "$OSSTACK_HOME/mission-view.json" \
82:   --wait WAIT_ID --action "$ANSWER_ID" --choice CHOICE_ID --json
83: 
84: osstack engineering observe --mission MISSION_ID --json
85: ```
86: 
87: 对于允许原文输入的问题，用 `--text "原始回答"` 代替 `--choice`。两者不能同时提供。系统不把“继续”自动猜成某个选项；文本也不能批准风险动作。
88: 
89: `--observation` 使用文件中原封不动的 Mission revision、问题引用和 token。CLI 不会在提交前偷偷刷新版本。宿主重新核对来源、身份、权限和过期时间。
90: 
91: 状态必须按证据理解：
92: 
93: | 状态或事实 | 含义 |
94: |---|---|
95: | 回答已记录 / reserved | 已持久预留回答；尚未证明 Worker 执行 |
96: | dispatch_committed | 已承诺这一次派发；结果不明时不能自动再发 |
97: | applied | 整个回复回合已完成并落库；不只是传输接收 |
98: | delivery_unknown / needs_reconciliation | 证据不足；保留原回执与责任 |
99: | Mission completed + ObservationProof | 本次回答的精确回合已确认，当前没有新决定、未清义务、未决副作用或取消意图 |
100: 
101: 若回复又提出决定，重新 `observe` 并处理新 Wait。较早的 Run 终态、普通 `done` 文本或 Session 空闲不能单独使 Mission 成功。
102: 
103: ## 3. 暂缓、取消与结束观察
104: 
105: 暂缓只改变重查安排。它不提供回答或执行权限。
106: 
107: ```sh
108: osstack engineering defer --observation "$OSSTACK_HOME/mission-view.json" \
109:   --wait WAIT_ID --request defer-001 \
110:   --revisit-at 2026-10-26T12:00:00Z --reason "等待产品输入"
111: 
112: osstack engineering cancel --mission MISSION_ID --revision REVISION \
113:   --request cancel-001 --reason "停止本次工作"
114: 
115: osstack engineering end-observation --mission MISSION_ID --revision REVISION \
116:   --request end-001 --reason "不再观察此回复"
117: ```
118: 
119: `cancel` 持久记录停止意图，并通过独立停止通道请求中止。未知回答不会阻塞它。`stop_requested` 或 `stop_unknown` 都不是停止证明。在途动作可能仍然完成。
120: 
121: `end-observation` 结束本 Mission 的观察，阻止其后续首次派发。它不停止 Worker，不声称成功，也不抹去 committed/unknown 回执。原 attention 对账责任和会话发送守卫仍然有效。 若原回答仍在等待仓库 writer 准入，且持久证明确认它已是 `cancelled_before_dispatch`，宿主会释放这条尚未开始的排队工作。此处理不向已 committed 的 Worker 发送中止。
122: 
123: 对已证明未派发的取消，原回答的同键、同正文重放返回 `cancelled_before_dispatch`；改变原正文返回 `action_conflict`。Mission 终结后，换新 action_id 再回答返回 `mission_terminal`。旧 `session.send` 重放这条已取消消息时使用错误码 `cancelled_original_answer`。这些结果都不允许恢复或替换原回答。
124: 
125: | 操作 | 当前权限要求 |
126: |---|---|
127: | adopt | scope read + write；记录不可变 owner |
128: | decide / defer / dispose_wait | 同一 owner + 当前 read + write |
129: | cancel | 当前 scope read + 独立 stop，且是 owner 或显式 scope-stop operator |
130: | end-observation | owner + read；或 operator + read + stop |
131: 
132: 其他 writer 不能接管 owner。write 不隐含 stop。owner 写权被撤销后，只要 read 与独立 stop 权仍有效，就可取消。read 权被撤销时不能读取或操作这个 Mission；另行认证的 operator 负责恢复处置。凭据整体撤销后由另行认证的 scope-stop operator 恢复处置。
133: 
134: ## 4. 对账
135: 
136: 查询原动作。查询不会重新发送：
137: 
138: ```sh
139: osstack engineering reconcile --kind query_action \
140:   --mission MISSION_ID --action "$ANSWER_ID" --json
141: ```
142: 
143: 如果用户已在原会话处理问题，且旧 Wait 没有关联回答 action、没有未知在途副作用，可以明确撤出旧观察义务。先读取新快照，再提交：
144: 
145: ```sh
146: osstack engineering observe --mission MISSION_ID --json > "$OSSTACK_HOME/mission-view.json"
147: osstack engineering reconcile --kind dispose_wait \
148:   --observation "$OSSTACK_HOME/mission-view.json" --wait WAIT_ID --request dispose-001 \
149:   --disposition human_answered_out_of_band --reason "已在原会话明确处理"
150: ```
151: 
152: 另一个处置值是 `no_longer_relevant`。处置绑定精确旧 Wait 和当前观测摘要，是用户授权的观察目标调整。它不产生 applied、停止证明或新的执行权限。有关联回答的 Wait 必须查询原 action 或取消，不能用人工声明清除未知副作用。
153: 
154: ## 5. 接口与限制
155: 
156: 所有命令都接受 `--input FILE`，用严格请求 JSON 代替其余业务参数；可同时使用 `--json`。文件上限为 1 MiB，必须是普通文件且不是符号链接。未知字段、重复参数和 `actor`/权限声明会被拒绝。协议 Zod 源码和已生成 JSON Schema 位于 `packages/protocol/src/engineering.ts` 与 `packages/protocol/schemas/engineering-*.schema.json`。
157: 
158: 以 `--` 开头的文本值（例如 `--help`）必须放入 `--input FILE` 的完整请求 JSON；把 `answer` 写为 `{"kind":"text","text":"--help"}`。不要把这段 JSON 当作完整请求。普通 `--text -h` 可用，但 `--text --help` 会被识别为缺失参数。
159: 
160: `observe` 的 `--cursor` 是事件序号，`--limit` 为 1–100。检查返回的 cursor、has_more 与未决 Wait；增量事件不是完整等待视图的替代。保存含 token 的 JSON 后应限制文件权限。
161: 
162: | 错误 | 下一步 |
163: |---|---|
164: | permission_denied | 让宿主管理者核对当前身份与精确 scope 权限；不要在 JSON 中伪造 actor |
165: | stale_decision / source_changed | 重新观察；先查询原 action，确认没有已接受副作用后再提交新决定 |
166: | action_conflict / action_id_conflict | 保留原键和原正文；不同意图使用独立新请求 |
167: | delivery_unknown | 查询原 action；不要换键重发 |
168: | signed_approval_required | 使用该风险/任务的正式批准入口 |
169: | executor_not_qualified | 检查默认租户、Session 状态、原生 ID 与支持的 Adapter profile |
170: | not_dispatched_use_cancel | 取消严格未派发预留；不要把它伪装成未知已发送 |
171: | cancelled_original_answer | 原消息已明确取消且未派发；查询原回执，不换键重发 |
172: | authority_already_adopted | 该精确决定已被采纳；包括无回答就取消/结束观察的情况。使用原 Mission/Session 入口 |
173: | budget_exhausted | 宿主或 Mission 的持久上限已达到；检查账本，不改库重置。当前 home 累计最多 1000 个 Mission，包含终态记录 |
174: | engineering_busy | 待派发槽已满；稍后重试未改变的原请求，读取和停止仍可用 |
175: | mission_terminal | Mission 已终结；读取现有结果，不把重试变成新的目标或回答 |
176: | interaction_expired | 先查询原 action；未接受的回答需重新 observe 获取当前 token，再提交明确的新请求 |
177: | interaction_mismatch | 核对 principal/client 和原观察文件；不得手工拼接 token、Wait 或问题引用 |
178: | durability_required | 实际 daemon 写连接没有验证为 synchronous=FULL；修正宿主配置，不绕过持久性检查 |
179: | stop_attempt_budget_exhausted | 本 Mission 已接受 8 次停止尝试；原停止意图仍保留。查询原回执，交由宿主操作员处理，不能换键绕过上限 |
180: 
181: 宿主同时最多运行 16 个内存中的派发尝试，每页 watchdog 最多检查 16 个 reserved action 和 16 个活跃 Mission。回复有 5 分钟宿主 deadline；到期请求中止，不代表进程已停止。每次暂缓最多产生一个提醒事件，可通过 `observe` 读取；试点不发送外部通知。
182: 
183: 每个 home 最多存储 1,000 条 Mission，每个 Mission 最多 64 个 Wait。每个 Mission 最多 256 个一般控制请求。取消和结束观察独立于此计数；取消另有每个 Mission 最多 8 次的停止尝试上限，包含第一次。每次尝试保留原停止意图，只面向本 Mission 尚未解决的原 committed 消息。相同请求重放不增加计数。结束观察是单次终结操作。交互令牌五分钟到期，每个 Mission 最多保留 32 个活跃令牌。遇到 `budget_exhausted` 或 `stop_attempt_budget_exhausted` 时，不得删除未解决的证据来绕过限制。查询既有回执，并由主机操作员核对额度。
184: 
185: `completed` 和 `failed` 是不可逆的终态。新的 cancel 或 end-observation 请求返回 `mission_terminal`，不会改写结果或中止同 Session 后续的手工指令。已接受的相同请求仍可只读重放。结束观察后若仍有本 Mission 的 committed 未决消息，独立停止通道可在上述停止额度内请求中止。
186: 
187: `cost_evidence` 明确返回 `status: unknown` 和 `hard_cap_enforced: false`。试点不核销 Provider 用量，不把缺失计量当作零。5 分钟宿主 deadline 不是 token 或费用硬上限。真实编程回复可能超过这个期限；到期中止可能留下不确定回执和部分工作区改动。此 profile 尚不适合不设时长边界的长回合任务。
188: 
189: ### 关闭功能后的处理
190: 
191: 关闭 `OSSTACK_ENGINEERING` 不会删除派发阶段或释放 Session 守卫。已有 `reserved` 行仍会阻止新的手工指令；旧 `abandon-receipt` 会要求先取消。工程入口关闭时不能通过它取消，因此须在相同 home 重新启用 `OSSTACK_ENGINEERING=1` 与 `OSSTACK_ATTENTION=1`，使用有效凭据查询并处理原 action，然后再关闭。不要改写或删除数据库回执来解锁。已 committed/unknown 的动作仍需原有对账，不能因重新启用而重发。
192: 
193: ### 升级、回退与恢复服务
194: 
195: schema 47 是数据库迁移边界。迁移完成后，只支持旧 schema 的 binary 不能打开这个 home。不能只退回旧 npm 版本或替换 binary；本试点没有受支持的数据库降级/自动回退通道。备份也不等于已获得回退许可：还须保留迁移后的动作和外部副作用证据。
196: 
197: 升级前会检查 `attention_dispatch_holds`。只有原 committed 消息已经 `applied`，或完整证明确认 `cancelled_before_dispatch`，才能清除相应 hold。committed 但未 applied 的消息持续阻止升级；failed、unknown、`abandoned_unknown` 回执、结束观察和关闭功能均不能清除它。对于没有原 applied 证明的失败或未知动作，这个限制可能无限期保留。当前试点没有自动解除它的受支持流程。保留原 home、原账本和可用副作用证据，等待单独设计并审核的离线处置方案；不要手工修改 phase、消息、回执或 schema version，也不要删除数据库来强制升级。
198: 
199: `daemon.drain` 即使返回 `drained: false`，daemon 仍处于 draining 状态。新的任务和会话写入会被拒绝；升级中止不会自动恢复接收工作。先确认没有安装、重启或其他维护仍在进行，再查询 hold。仍是严格未派发 reserved 的回答可在 draining 状态下显式取消；已经 committed 的未知动作不能据此释放。
200: 
201: 如果决定放弃本次升级并继续运行当前版本，由受信宿主管理者调用 `daemon.resume` RPC，然后核对 `osstack health` 的 `result.draining` 为 `false`。本版没有 `osstack daemon resume` 或通用 `osstack rpc` 子命令。下面的 Node 命令使用现有本地 socket RPC；它不打开数据库。必须先把 `OSSTACK_SOCKET` 换成启动日志中该 home 的精确绝对 socket 路径，并保持 `OSSTACK_HOME` 指向该 home：
202: 
203: ```sh
204: export OSSTACK_SOCKET=/absolute/path/from-daemon-startup.sock
205: node --input-type=module <<'NODE'
206: import { connect } from 'node:net';
207: import { isAbsolute } from 'node:path';
208: const path = process.env.OSSTACK_SOCKET;
209: if (!path || !isAbsolute(path)) throw Error('Set OSSTACK_SOCKET to the exact absolute daemon socket path');
210: const socket = connect(path);
211: let bytes = Buffer.alloc(0), done = false;
212: const fail = message => { if (done) return; done = true; clearTimeout(timer); socket.destroy(); console.error(message); process.exitCode = 1; };
213: const timer = setTimeout(() => fail('daemon.resume timed out'), 5000);
214: socket.on('error', error => fail(error.message));
215: socket.on('end', () => { if (!done) fail('Incomplete RPC response'); });
216: socket.on('connect', () => {
217:   const body = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ id: 1, method: 'daemon.resume' }));
218:   const header = Buffer.alloc(4); header.writeUInt32BE(body.length);
219:   socket.write(Buffer.concat([header, body]));
220: });
221: socket.on('data', chunk => {
222:   bytes = Buffer.concat([bytes, chunk]);
223:   if (bytes.length < 4) return;
224:   const size = bytes.readUInt32BE(0);
225:   if (size > 1048576) return fail('RPC response exceeds 1 MiB');
226:   if (bytes.length < size + 4) return;
227:   try {
228:     const response = JSON.parse(bytes.subarray(4, size + 4).toString('utf8'));
229:     if (response.id !== 1 || response.ok !== true || response.result?.draining !== false) throw Error('Resume was not confirmed');
230:     done = true; clearTimeout(timer); socket.end(); console.log(JSON.stringify(response));
231:   } catch (error) { fail(error.message); }
232: });
233: NODE
234: osstack health
235: ```
236: 
237: 恢复接收工作会让符合条件的队列继续调度，必须先处理不希望执行的 reserved 回答。`daemon.resume` 不解除 upgrade hold，不证明未知动作已停止，也不是数据库回退。若磁盘等健康条件仍不满足，健康检查可能再次进入 draining。以上 RPC 属于现有受信本地宿主接口，并非 engineering 凭据授权接口。
238: 
239: 尚需独立验证：真实 Provider 回答、物理断电与文件系统持久性、跨重启的进程域停止证明、恶意同 UID Worker 隔离、非默认租户、远程宿主。单元测试、完整测试和模型审计不能替代这些资格。
240: 
241: 完整规格见[修订后的运行时契约](../design/2026-10-25-engineering-agent/runtime-contract-v2.md)与[试点场景](../design/2026-10-25-engineering-agent/pilot-scenarios.json)。

===== FILE docs/design/2026-10-25-engineering-agent/evidence/sonnet-5.5/disposition.md sha256=f5f1b6b8f9fec8360139eb31df915deedc296018c3eefae16cd7ffd966a2ce72 =====
1: # Sonnet 5.5 实现审计处置
2: 
3: ## 方法与范围
4: 
5: Claude Code CLI 实际使用 `claude-sonnet-5-5`，无工具、无 MCP、无持久 session，对冻结输入进行静态审计。原始 JSON 的 `modelUsage` 确认模型，不以 prompt 中自称作为证明。测试由实现团队执行；Claude 没有执行测试。本报告不是生产资格认证。
6: 
7: 两份首轮审计分别检查完整采纳流程与升级兼容。最终复审检查下面的处置和更新源码。每轮保留输入、逐文件 SHA-256 manifest、原始 JSON 输出；不重写历史审计。
8: 
9: ## 逐项处置
10: 
11: | 首轮编号 | 处置 | 实现与验证 |
12: |---|---|---|
13: | P1-1 正常在途回复被降为 unknown | 修复 | host 注入当前进程的精确 live message；refresh/replay 显示 in_flight；完成事务同时更新 action/item。超过两个 tick 的真实 daemon fixture 与重启未知测试分开验证 |
14: | P1-2 瞬时来源失败永久污染 Wait | 修复 | 临时 IO 保留 Wait 并记录去重原因；来源身份改变继续阻断；未分类程序错误回滚。目录恢复和 TypeError 零写测试 |
15: | P1-3 migration 47 表谱系未展示 | 补证，无需另建手工登记 | db.ts 从 MIGRATIONS[].sql 动态识别 CREATE TABLE；提供完整源码及 47 重新打开、逐一缺失 7 张表拒绝测试。未来 48 未实现且保持拒绝，不伪造未来兼容 |
16: | P2-1 拒绝动作反复占用 admission | 修复 | admission 前纯读预检、异步 admission 后重新验证、1 秒至 60 秒退避、每种原因最多一条持久事件 |
17: | P2-2 终态可改写、取消误停后续消息、停止次数无界 | 修复 | completed/failed 为吸收态；保留首个停止意图；停止仅匹配 Mission 原消息；最多 8 次 stop 请求。另对严格证明未派发的原排队消息释放 admission，不触及后续人工发送 |
18: | P2-3 对象存在性探测 | 修复 | 未知或无 read 权的 Mission 统一 permission_denied，先检 scope 再使用仓库元数据 |
19: | P2-3 全局 action_id | 保留现有合约并明确限制 | attention action/message 是全局共享发送权限；本轮不引入第二套 ID 映射。CLI 指南使用全局唯一 UUID。重复 ID 发生严格冲突并回滚，不读出另一 Mission 回执，也不重发。两个客户端故意复用 ID 仍会冲突；不是多租户命名空间隔离 |
20: | P2-4 取消回执在旧视图显示未知 | 修复 | read model 使用严格取消证明；坏证明显示 dispatch_inconsistent；session.send 对原消息返回 cancelled_original_answer，排空期间同样可读取 |
21: | P2-5 空闲轮询反复 FULL 写 | 修复 | observed_at 最多每分钟更新；action state 不变不更新；正常空闲与 unknown 空闲 total_changes 回归测试 |
22: | P2-6 一次采纳、容量和错误说明 | 有界试点限制，已修订契约和指南 | 同一原决定即使未回答且结束观察，也不能再次采纳；全库累计 1000 Mission，无自动回收；原答案重放返回取消状态，新请求返回 mission_terminal；完整错误表 |
23: | P2-7 决策摘要口径 | 修复 | decide/defer/dispatch/refresh 都对 SessionDecision.parse 后内容取摘要；带 trim/default 规范化的测试 |
24: | P2-8 回滚、时限与凭据 | 文档明确限制，CLI 缺陷修复 | feature off 不抹掉 reserved 权限，恢复取消需重新启用；5 分钟仅请求 abort，费用仍 unknown；轮换凭据不等于撤销 principal/client grant；答案文本 -h 不再触发 help |
25: | 升级 P2-1 取消与未知 phase 放行 | 修复 | 纯读共享 guard 仅允许 committed+applied 或严格有效的取消证明；真实 schema 47 及破坏证据矩阵 |
26: | 升级 P2-2 旧 schema 藏新表 | 修复 | schema 小于 47 却有 phase 表时拒绝未知谱系，既不跳过也不擅自迁移 |
27: | 升级 P2-3 停服后才发现未完成动作 | 修复 | daemon.drain 返回 attention_dispatch_holds，进入 drain 后不再派发；有 holds 时在 stop 前拒绝，停止后在 DB 独占锁内再次检查 |
28: 
29: ## 保留边界
30: 
31: - 旧本地 socket 路由仍属于 trusted-process 管理面；engineering 的凭据不把同 UID 的恶意程序隔离出来。
32: - committed 但未 applied 的未知消息不因人工放弃回执而自动放行升级；明确保留不确定性，不能据此声称已停止。
33: - 两个独立进程 SIGKILL 场景覆盖派发前与已调用后；事务故障注入另测回滚，不声称覆盖物理掉电或所有指令窗口。
34: - 真实受控 Provider 需要独立 API 凭据。Claude Code CLI 审计登录成功不等于该 Adapter 可执行真实 Session。
35: - 本次只交付 existing Session 的 observed_turn，不交付自动初始 Worker、Loop/Graph 选路或 24 项能力全面运行时融合。
36: 
37: ## 最终复审
38: 
39: 第二轮原文：[JSON](sonnet-round2.json) · [可读报告](sonnet-round2.md)。结论为受限本地试点通过、无 P0/P1，同时提出三项 P2；继续整改如下，并提交第三轮聚焦复审。
40: 
41: | 第二轮编号 | 处置 |
42: |---|---|
43: | P2-A 完成事务依赖 realpath | 完成事务只读取 DB 内的 task/run/native/scope/source 绑定及最新 snapshot，不调用文件系统，也不在事务内 refresh。实际 turn、applied、completion proof 原子保存；后续观察才确认 Mission completed。真实目录 rename 消失与三种 DB 身份变化测试区分验证。 |
44: | P2-B drained:false 被 literal schema 吞成通用错误 | 先解析非负计数与布尔值，优先报告 attention hold，再检查 graph/active/drained。fake fixture 与真实 daemon 都返回 drained:false、hold:1。拒绝后保持 draining 是现有协调器行为；指南给出确认升级已中止后的显式恢复方法。 |
45: | P2-C 无法升级或回退的运维边界 | 中英文指南与 ADR-16 明写：committed 未 applied 始终阻止升级，包括人工 abandon；本试点没有受支持的自动清除/离线放行实现。schema47 无法由旧 binary 打开。保留证据，禁止重置 user_version 或直接改账本。 |
46: | P3-1 纯未派发取消仍显示 stop_unknown | 只有存在 committed 未完成消息时写停止未知事件；纯已证明未派发的取消保留原证据并释放本 Mission 排队 admission。 |
47: | P3-2 旧 Session 错误分类 | session.send 将 dispatch_inconsistent 保留为明确错误码。 |
48: | P3-3 以 -- 开头文本 | 指南明确使用完整 --input JSON，避免把值当作选项。 |
49: | P3-4 scope reader 的资格信息 | 当前 scope reader 可获知该 scope Session 的资格状态，这是受信本地可读信息；不新增跨 scope 权限。 |
50: 
51: 第三轮静态复审结果与最终执行凭据在完成后追加。

755:     expected_version: p["expected_version"] as number,
756:     text: p["text"] as string,
757:     ...(p["requested_by"] === undefined ? {} : { requested_by: p["requested_by"] as string }),
758:   };
759: }

761: async function handleSessionSend(
762:   ctx: DaemonContext,
763:   req: DaemonRequest,
764: ): Promise<SingleResponse> {
765:   const p = parseSessionSendParams(req.params);
766:   if (p instanceof Error) return singleErr(req.id, "bad_request", p.message);
767:   // Completed receipt replay remains read-only while drained; no new/retried
768:   // instruction may reserve a message or invoke an adapter during an upgrade.
769:   if (ctx.draining && ctx.sessions.getMessage(p.message_id)?.state !== "applied" && !isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(ctx.db, p.message_id)) {
770:     return singleErr(req.id, "draining", "daemon is not accepting session instructions");
771:   }
772: 
773:   const session = ctx.sessions.getSession(p.session_id);
774:   if (session === undefined) return singleErr(req.id, "not_found", "session not found");
775:   if (p.runtime_id !== session.agent_id) {
776:     return singleErr(req.id, "runtime_mismatch", "session belongs to another runtime");
777:   }
778:   // Idempotency wins over moving state: a retry may arrive after the first
779:   // delivery already appended a newer turn or completed the conversation.
780:   // Let the repository compare the complete canonical envelope before any
781:   // current-turn/current-state guards reject it.
782:   const existingMessage = ctx.sessions.getMessage(p.message_id);
783:   if (existingMessage !== undefined) {
784:     try {
785:       const replay = ctx.sessions.applyInbound({
786:         message_id: p.message_id,
787:         session_id: p.session_id,
788:         turn_id: p.turn_id,
789:         direction: "inbound",
790:         kind: "instruction.send",
791:         payload: { text: p.text, ...(p.requested_by === undefined ? {} : { requested_by: p.requested_by }) },
792:         state: "accepted",
793:         created_at: Date.now() / 1000,
794:       }, p.expected_version, Date.now() / 1000);
795:       if (isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(ctx.db, p.message_id)) {
796:         return singleErr(req.id, "cancelled_original_answer", "The original answer was cancelled before dispatch. It cannot be sent again.");
797:       }
798:       if (existingMessage.state === "applied") {
799:         return singleOk(req.id, { state: replay.outcome, session_version: replay.session_version });
800:       }
801:       if (replay.session_version !== p.expected_version + 1) {
802:         return singleErr(req.id, "version_conflict", "accepted message cannot be recovered safely");
803:       }
804:       if (existingMessage.state === "accepted") {
805:         return ctx.activeSessionSends.has(session.session_id)
806:           ? singleErr(req.id, "session_busy", "session is already applying another instruction")
807:           : singleErr(
808:               req.id,
809:               "delivery_uncertain",
810:               "the previous delivery may have reached the agent; inspect the session before using a new message_id",
811:             );
812:       }
813:       if (existingMessage.state !== "failed") {
814:         return singleErr(req.id, "version_conflict", "reserved message is not retryable");
815:       }
816:       return singleErr(req.id, "delivery_uncertain", "Failed execution may have had side effects; reconcile the existing receipt before retrying");
817:     } catch (err) {
818:       if (err instanceof SessionMessageIdConflict) {
819:         return singleErr(req.id, "message_id_conflict", "message_id was already used for different content");
820:       }
821:       throw err;
822:     }
823:   }
824:   if (TERMINAL_SESSION_STATES.has(session.state)) {
825:     return singleErr(req.id, "session_terminal", `session state=${session.state}`);
826:   }
827:   if (!session.resume_supported || session.native_session_id === null) {
828:     return singleErr(req.id, "resume_unsupported", "session cannot be resumed");
829:   }
830:   const snapshot = ctx.sessions.latestSnapshot(p.session_id);
831:   if (snapshot.turn_id !== p.turn_id) {
832:     return singleErr(req.id, "turn_conflict", "message does not target the current turn");
833:   }
834:   const adapter = ctx.adapters.get(session.agent_id);
835:   if (adapter === undefined || !isSessionCapableAdapter(adapter) || !adapter.sessionCapabilities().resume) {
836:     return singleErr(req.id, "resume_unsupported", "agent does not support resume");
837:   }
838:   if (ctx.activeSessionSends.has(session.session_id)) {
839:     return singleErr(req.id, "session_busy", "session is already applying another instruction");
840:   }
841: 
842:   // Refuse busy/denied admission before minting an instruction receipt.
843:   let admission: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof acquireManualSessionAdmission>>;
844:   try { admission = await acquireManualSessionAdmission(ctx, session, p.message_id); }
845:   catch { return singleErr(req.id, "admission_denied", "Session execution admission was denied; no instruction was reserved"); }
846:   let handedOff = false;
847:   try {
848:     if (ctx.draining || admission.controller.signal.aborted) return singleErr(req.id, "admission_denied", "Session execution was cancelled; no instruction was reserved");
849:     let applied: { outcome: "applied" | "duplicate"; session_version: number };
850:     const now = Date.now() / 1000;
851:     try {
852:       applied = reserveSessionInstruction(ctx.db, ctx.dbPath, {
853:         message_id: p.message_id,
854:         session_id: p.session_id,
855:         turn_id: p.turn_id,
856:         direction: "inbound",
857:         kind: "instruction.send",
858:         payload: { text: p.text, ...(p.requested_by === undefined ? {} : { requested_by: p.requested_by }) },
859:         state: "accepted",
860:         created_at: now,
861:       }, p.expected_version, now);
862:     } catch (err) {
863:       if (err instanceof SessionVersionConflict) {
864:         return singleErr(req.id, "version_conflict", "session version changed; refresh before retrying");
865:       }
866:       if (err instanceof SessionMessageIdConflict) {
867:         return singleErr(req.id, "message_id_conflict", "message_id was already used for different content");
868:       }
869:       if (err instanceof Error && ["delivery_uncertain", "continuation_in_flight", "grant_owned", "signed_approval_required", "invalid_decision", "dispatch_inconsistent"].includes(err.message)) return singleErr(req.id, err.message, "Refresh the decision and use its authorized owner route");
870:       throw err;
871:     }
872:     if (applied.outcome === "duplicate") {
873:       return ctx.sessions.getMessage(p.message_id)?.state === "applied"
874:         ? singleOk(req.id, { state: "duplicate", session_version: applied.session_version })
875:         : singleErr(req.id, "delivery_uncertain", "A receipt appeared during admission; inspect it before retrying");
876:     }
877: 
878:     handedOff = true;
879:     return await executeReservedSessionTurn(ctx, req, p, session, adapter, applied.session_version, { admittedLease: admission.lease, controller: admission.controller });
880:   } finally { if (!handedOff) await admission.release(); }
881: 
882: }
883: 
884: /** Track queued manual work before awaiting admission so drain/cancel can stop it.
885:  * No durable receipt exists until admission succeeds; callers transfer both
886:  * ownership and the same cancellation signal to the reserved-turn executor.
887:  */
888: async function acquireManualSessionAdmission(
889:   ctx: DaemonContext,
890:   session: NonNullable<ReturnType<SessionRepository["getSession"]>>,
891:   pendingMessageId: string,
892: ) {
893:   const controller = new AbortController();
894:   ctx.activeSessionSends.set(session.session_id, pendingMessageId);
895:   ctx.activeRuns.set(session.run_id, controller);
896:   const clear = () => {
897:     if (ctx.activeSessionSends.get(session.session_id) === pendingMessageId) ctx.activeSessionSends.delete(session.session_id);
898:     if (ctx.activeRuns.get(session.run_id) === controller) ctx.activeRuns.delete(session.run_id);
899:   };
900:   try {
901:     const lease = await acquireSessionAdmission(ctx, session.agent_id, session.repo_path, session.run_id, controller.signal);
902:     return { lease, controller, release: async () => { try { await lease.release(); } finally { clear(); } } };
903:   } catch (error) {
904:     clear();
905:     throw error;
906:   }
907: }
908: 
909: /** Private port: only an already persisted reservation may invoke the runtime. */
910: async function executeReservedSessionTurn(
911:   ctx: DaemonContext,
912:   req: DaemonRequest,
913:   p: SessionSendParams,
914:   session: NonNullable<ReturnType<SessionRepository["getSession"]>>,
915:   adapter: SessionCapableAdapter,
916:   reservationVersion: number,
917:   options: {
918:     admittedLease?: ExecutionLease;
919:     controller?: AbortController;
920:     completed?: (turnId: string, version: number, now: number) => void;
921:     engineeringPermit?: Parameters<import("@osstack/core").EngineeringStore["consumeDispatchPermit"]>[0];
922:   } = {},
923: ): Promise<SingleResponse> {
924:   const applied = { session_version: reservationVersion };
925:   ctx.activeSessionSends.set(session.session_id,p.message_id);
926:   const controller = options.controller ?? new AbortController();
927:   const now = Date.now() / 1000;
928:   ctx.activeRuns.set(session.run_id, controller);
929:   let lease: ExecutionLease | undefined = options.admittedLease;
930:   let started = false;
931:   let settled = false;
932:   try {
933:     // This guard stays active even when the feature flag is off. An extended
934:     // receipt can enter this helper only with the one-use CAS winner's permit.
935:     const engineeringPhase = ctx.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_dispatch_phases WHERE message_id=?").get(p.message_id);
936:     if (engineeringPhase) {
937:       if (!ctx.engineering || !options.engineeringPermit) return singleErr(req.id, "dispatch_gate_required", "Use the original engineering action; this receipt cannot be sent through another route");
938:       try { ctx.engineering.store.consumeDispatchPermit(options.engineeringPermit, p.message_id); }
939:       catch { return singleErr(req.id, "dispatch_gate_required", "The dispatch permit is invalid or already consumed"); }
940:     }

1049:     const updatedSession = ctx.db.transaction(() => {

1056:           ).get(session.session_id) as { ordinal: number }).ordinal,

1058:           assistant_text: assistantText,

1073:       session_version: updatedSession.version,

1075:     });
1076:   } catch {

1081:     return singleErr(req.id, started ? "delivery_uncertain" : "admission_denied", "Session execution could not be confirmed; inspect persisted state before retrying");
1082:   } finally {

1084:       if (started && (!settled || controller.signal.aborted)) {
1085:         controller.abort();

1087:         else lease?.retain("session resume exit unconfirmed");
1088:       } else await lease?.release();
1089:     } finally {
1090:       ctx.activeSessionSends.delete(session.session_id);
1091:       if (ctx.activeRuns.get(session.run_id) === controller) ctx.activeRuns.delete(session.run_id);
1092:       requestSupervisionScan(ctx, "session.send.settled");
1093:     }
1094:   }
1095: }

1097: function requestSupervisionScan(ctx: DaemonContext, reason: string): void {
1098:   if (ctx.draining || !ctx.supervision) return;

1100:     // The periodic pass is the recovery path; never fail a persisted session reply.
1101:   });

1108: async function getAttentionTriage(ctx: DaemonContext) {
1109:   if (!ctx.supervision || ctx.draining) throw new Error("attention_unavailable");
1110:   ctx.attentionTriage ??= (async () => {

1115:     const model = enabled ? process.env["OSSTACK_ATTENTION_TRIAGE_MODEL"] ?? "" : "disabled";
1116:     const maxCost = enabled ? Number(process.env["OSSTACK_ATTENTION_TRIAGE_MAX_COST_USD"] ?? 0.05) : 0.05;
1117:     if (!Number.isFinite(maxCost) || maxCost <= 0 || maxCost > 0.25) throw new Error("invalid_triage_configuration");
1118:     try {

1120:       ledger:new AttentionTriageLedger(ctx.supervision.store,ctx.supervision.observer.owner),
1121:       config:{enabled,model_id:model,calls_per_hour:enabled ? Number(process.env["OSSTACK_ATTENTION_TRIAGE_CALLS_PER_HOUR"] ?? 5) : 5,
1122:         timeout_ms:enabled ? Number(process.env["OSSTACK_ATTENTION_TRIAGE_TIMEOUT_MS"] ?? 20000) : 20000},

1127:       }),
1128:     });

1130:     } catch { throw new Error("invalid_triage_configuration"); }

1132:   return ctx.attentionTriage;
